# MADVENTURE PROJECT SPECIFICATION INDEX

This file contains several pasted implementation drafts. The repository source is authoritative; this file is an ordered merge reference only.

## Canonical Merge Order

1. `package.json`, `vite.config.js`, `index.html`, `.env.example`
2. `src/main.jsx`, `src/index.css`, `src/App.jsx`
3. `src/lib/*`, `src/context/*`, `src/hooks/*`
4. `src/services/*`, `src/models/*`, `src/utils/*`
5. `src/components/*`
6. `src/pages/*`, `src/features/*`, `src/admin/*`
7. `supabase/migrations/*`, then standalone SQL patches

## Duplicate-Path Rule

When a path appears more than once, use the latest block that matches the current repository architecture. Do not blindly replace the existing app with the standalone draft. The current app uses `profiles`, `places`, `tour_plans`, and HTML `blog_posts.content`; drafts that require `blog_authors`, `blog_categories`, `trip_plans`, or block-array content need an explicit migration and adapter first.

Known duplicate paths: `package.json`, `vite.config.js`, `index.html`, `src/App.jsx`, `src/index.css`, `src/main.jsx`, `src/lib/supabase.js`, `src/config/navigation.jsx`, `src/components/layout/Sidebar.jsx`, `src/components/layout/Topbar.jsx`, `src/services/blogService.js`, and `supabase/schema.sql`.

## Current Implemented Additions

- Destination Hub: `src/pages/DestinationHub.jsx`
- Article workflow: `src/pages/ArticleEditor.jsx`, `src/components/blog/BlockEditor.jsx`, `src/pages/MyArticles.jsx`, `src/pages/admin/AdminBlog.jsx`
- Blog workflow migration: `supabase/migrations/27_blog_editor_workflow.sql`

## Imported Resource Repositories

### Bangladesh GeoJSON

Source: `bangladesh-geojson/` from `https://github.com/ifahimreza/bangladesh-geojson`

Use these files for bilingual location search, filter dropdowns, Leaflet boundaries, and map labels:

- `bangladesh-geojson/src/data/bd-divisions.json`
- `bangladesh-geojson/src/data/bd-districts.json`
- `bangladesh-geojson/src/data/bd-upazilas.json`
- `bangladesh-geojson/src/data/bd-postcodes.json`
- `bangladesh-geojson/src/data/bangladesh.geojson`
- `bangladesh-geojson/src/data/bd-bazars.geojson`
- `bangladesh-geojson/src/data/bd-major-routes.geojson`

Do not embed the large GeoJSON files in this document. Copy or import them from the cloned repository into `src/data/bangladesh-geojson/` only when the map feature needs them. Preserve attribution: boundary data is CC BY 4.0; OSM-derived layers use ODbL 1.0 and require `© OpenStreetMap contributors — odbl-1.0`.

### Travel Skills

Source: `travel-skills/` from `https://github.com/stayingapi/travel-skills`

This repository contains agent/API documentation for stay search, availability, price comparison, review intelligence, and alerts. It is not a frontend data source. Use it only through a server-side integration. Never place `STAYINGAPI_KEY` in React, `VITE_*` client variables, or browser requests.

Recommended first skill: `travel-skills/skills/stayingapi-search/SKILL.md`.

## GeoJSON Location Adapter

When the cloned data is copied into the application, add this small adapter instead of duplicating location parsing throughout pages:

```text
==== FILE: src/services/locationService.js ====
import divisions from '../data/bangladesh-geojson/bd-divisions.json'
import districts from '../data/bangladesh-geojson/bd-districts.json'
import upazilas from '../data/bangladesh-geojson/bd-upazilas.json'

const text = value => String(value || '').toLowerCase().trim()

export function searchLocations(query = '') {
  const term = text(query)
  if (!term) return []
  const rows = [
    ...divisions.map(item => ({ ...item, level: 'division' })),
    ...districts.map(item => ({ ...item, level: 'district' })),
    ...upazilas.map(item => ({ ...item, level: 'upazila' })),
  ]
  return rows.filter(item => text(item.name).includes(term) || text(item.bn_name).includes(term))
}

export function getDistrictsByDivision(divisionId) {
  return districts.filter(item => String(item.division_id) === String(divisionId))
}

export function getUpazilasByDistrict(districtId) {
  return upazilas.filter(item => String(item.district_id) === String(districtId))
}
==== END ====
```

The adapter is local and deterministic, so it can power the search box before Supabase results arrive. Use the selected location name as the query for `GoogleMapPreview`; use `lat`/`long` when rendering Leaflet markers.

## Server-Side Stay Skills Integration

Only add this when live accommodation comparison is requested. The API call belongs in `server/`, not in a client component:

```text
==== FILE: server/services/stayingApi.js ====
export async function searchStays(payload) {
  const response = await fetch('https://api.stayingapi.com/v1/search', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${process.env.STAYINGAPI_KEY}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
  })
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`StayingAPI request failed: ${response.status}`)
  return response.json()
}
==== END ====
```

Required environment variable: `STAYINGAPI_KEY` in the server environment only. Add rate limiting, timeout handling, and provider attribution before exposing this through a public route.

## Validation

Run `npm run lint` and `npm run build` after applying a block. Run Supabase migrations in filename order. Never run every SQL code block in this document as one script without checking `CREATE TABLE`, policy, and column conflicts.

## 📦 নতুন `project.txt` (আগেরটার সাথে এগুলো replace/add — installer নিজে merge করে দেবে)

```text
==== FILE: src/hooks/useAuthorRole.js ====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'

let cache = { role: null, loaded: false }

export default function useAuthorRole() {
  const [state, setState] = useState(cache)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (cache.loaded) return
    ;(async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
      if (!user) {
        cache = { role: null, loaded: true }
      } else {
        const { data } = await supabase
          .from('blog_authors')
          .select('author_type')
          .eq('user_id', user.id)
          .maybeSingle()
        cache = { role: data?.author_type || 'community_writer', loaded: true }
      }
      setState(cache)
    })()
  }, [])

  const role = state.role
  return {
    role,
    loading: !state.loaded,
    isStaff: ['admin', 'editor', 'staff_writer'].includes(role),
    isAdmin: role === 'admin',
  }
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/services/articleService.js ====
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'

export const slugify = t => (t || '').toLowerCase().trim()
  .replace(/[^a-z0-9\u0980-\u09FF]+/g, '-').replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '')

export async function fetchMyPosts() {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) throw new Error('login-required')
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('blog_posts')
    .select(`id, title, slug, excerpt, status, views_count, rejection_reason,
             created_at, published_at, cover_image_url, blog_categories(name)`)
    .eq('author_id', user.id)
    .order('updated_at', { ascending: false })
  if (error) throw error
  return data || []
}

export async function fetchPostForEdit(id) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('blog_posts')
    .select('*, blog_categories(id, name, slug)')
    .eq('id', id).single()
  if (error) throw error
  return data
}

export async function uploadTo(bucket, file) {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  const path = `${user?.id || 'anon'}/${Date.now()}-${file.name.replace(/\s+/g, '-')}`
  const { error } = await supabase.storage.from(bucket).upload(path, file)
  if (error) throw error
  return supabase.storage.from(bucket).getPublicUrl(path).data.publicUrl
}

export async function savePost(payload, id = null) {
  if (id) {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('blog_posts').update(payload).eq('id', id).select().single()
    if (error) throw error
    return data
  }
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('blog_posts').insert(payload).select().single()
  if (error) throw error
  return data
}

export async function setPostStatus(id, status, extra = {}) {
  const patch = { status, ...extra }
  if (status === 'published') patch.published_at = new Date().toISOString()
  const { error } = await supabase.from('blog_posts').update(patch).eq('id', id)
  if (error) throw error
}

export async function deleteMyPost(id) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('blog_posts').delete().eq('id', id)
  if (error) throw error
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/services/adminService.js ====
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'

export async function fetchAdminStats() {
  const counts = {}
  for (const s of ['published', 'draft', 'review', 'scheduled', 'archived']) {
    const { count } = await supabase.from('blog_posts')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('status', s)
    counts[s] = count ?? 0
  }
  const { count: total } = await supabase.from('blog_posts')
    .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
  const { data: vrows } = await supabase.from('blog_posts').select('views_count').limit(2000)
  const views = (vrows || []).reduce((a, b) => a + (b.views_count || 0), 0)
  const { count: pendingReports } = await supabase.from('blog_reports')
    .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('status', 'pending')
  const { count: comments } = await supabase.from('blog_comments')
    .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
  return { total: total ?? 0, views, pendingReports: pendingReports ?? 0, comments: comments ?? 0, ...counts }
}

export async function fetchAllPosts(status = null) {
  let q = supabase.from('blog_posts')
    .select(`id, title, slug, status, is_featured, views_count, created_at, published_at,
             scheduled_at, rejection_reason,
             blog_authors(display_name, avatar_url), blog_categories(name)`)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(200)
  if (status) q = q.eq('status', status)
  const { data, error } = await q
  if (error) throw error
  return data || []
}

export async function adminSetStatus(id, status, extra = {}) {
  const patch = { status, ...extra }
  if (status === 'published') patch.published_at = new Date().toISOString()
  const { error } = await supabase.from('blog_posts').update(patch).eq('id', id)
  if (error) throw error
}

export async function toggleFeatured(p) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('blog_posts')
    .update({ is_featured: !p.is_featured, featured_order: p.is_featured ? null : 1 })
    .eq('id', p.id)
  if (error) throw error
}

export async function adminDeletePost(id) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('blog_posts').delete().eq('id', id)
  if (error) throw error
}

export async function fetchReports() {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('blog_reports')
    .select('*, blog_posts(title, slug), blog_comments(content)')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(100)
  if (error) throw error
  return data || []
}

export async function resolveReport(id, status) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('blog_reports')
    .update({ status, reviewed_by: (await supabase.auth.getUser()).data.user?.id })
    .eq('id', id)
  if (error) throw error
}

export async function fetchRecentComments() {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('blog_comments')
    .select('*, blog_posts(title, slug), blog_authors(display_name, avatar_url)')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(50)
  if (error) throw error
  return data || []
}

export async function removeComment(id) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('blog_comments')
    .update({ status: 'removed' }).eq('id', id)
  if (error) throw error
}

export async function fetchAuthors() {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('blog_authors')
    .select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(100)
  if (error) throw error
  return data || []
}

export async function setAuthorType(userId, type) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('blog_authors')
    .update({ author_type: type }).eq('user_id', userId)
  if (error) throw error
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/components/blog/BlockRenderer.jsx ====
export const slugify = t => (t || '').toLowerCase()
  .replace(/[^a-z0-9\u0980-\u09FF]+/g, '-').replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '')

function Block({ b }) {
  switch (b.type) {
    case 'heading': {
      const Tag = `h${b.level || 2}`
      return <Tag id={slugify(b.text)} className={`${b.level === 2 ? 'text-2xl' : 'text-xl'} font-bold text-ink mt-8 mb-3`}>{b.text}</Tag>
    }
    case 'paragraph': return <p className="text-body leading-relaxed mb-4">{b.text}</p>
    case 'quote': return <blockquote className="border-l-4 border-cta bg-cta/5 pl-4 pr-4 py-3 italic text-body my-5 rounded-r-xl">{b.text}</blockquote>
    case 'list': {
      const List = b.ordered ? 'ol' : 'ul'
      return <List className={`${b.ordered ? 'list-decimal' : 'list-disc'} pl-6 text-body space-y-1.5 mb-4`}>
        {(b.items || []).map((it, i) => <li key={i}>{it}</li>)}
      </List>
    }
    case 'image': return (
      <figure className="my-6">
        <img src={b.url} alt={b.caption || ''} className="rounded-2xl w-full" />
        {b.caption && <figcaption className="text-sm text-muted text-center mt-2">{b.caption}</figcaption>}
      </figure>
    )
    default: return null
  }
}

export default function BlockRenderer({ blocks = [] }) {
  return blocks.map((b, i) => <Block key={i} b={b} />)
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/components/blog/BlockEditor.jsx ====
import { useRef } from 'react'
import {
  Heading2, Type, Quote, List, ListOrdered, ImagePlus,
  ArrowUp, ArrowDown, Trash2, Upload,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase'
import { uploadTo } from '../../services/articleService'

let uid = 0
const newBlock = type => {
  const base = { id: ++uid, type }
  if (type === 'heading') return { ...base, level: 2, text: '' }
  if (type === 'paragraph' || type === 'quote') return { ...base, text: '' }
  if (type === 'list') return { ...base, ordered: false, items: [''] }
  if (type === 'image') return { ...base, url: '', caption: '' }
  return base
}

const TOOLS = [
  { type: 'heading', icon: Heading2, label: 'Heading' },
  { type: 'paragraph', icon: Type, label: 'Text' },
  { type: 'quote', icon: Quote, label: 'Quote' },
  { type: 'list', icon: List, label: 'List' },
  { type: 'image', icon: ImagePlus, label: 'Image' },
]

export default function BlockEditor({ blocks, onChange }) {
  const fileRefs = useRef({})

  const add = type => onChange([...blocks, newBlock(type)])
  const update = (i, patch) => onChange(blocks.map((b, x) => (x === i ? { ...b, ...patch } : b)))
  const move = (i, d) => {
    const j = i + d
    if (j < 0 || j >= blocks.length) return
    const n = [...blocks]
    ;[n[i], n[j]] = [n[j], n[i]]
    onChange(n)
  }
  const remove = i => onChange(blocks.filter((_, x) => x !== i))

  async function handleImage(i, file) {
    if (!file) return
    try { update(i, { url: await uploadTo('blog-media', file) }) }
    catch (e) { alert('Upload failed: ' + e.message) }
  }

  const inputCls = 'w-full bg-soft rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand/20'

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Add-block toolbar */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {TOOLS.map(t => (
          <button key={t.type} onClick={() => add(t.type)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-soft text-body text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-brand-soft hover:text-brand transition">
            <t.icon size={13} /> {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3 mt-4">
        {blocks.map((b, i) => (
          <div key={b.id} className="border border-line rounded-2xl p-3 bg-card">
            {/* Block header */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mb-2">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted mr-auto">
                {b.type === 'heading' ? `H${b.level}` : b.type}
              </span>
              {b.type === 'heading' && (
                <select value={b.level || 2} onChange={e => update(i, { level: +e.target.value })}
                  className="text-xs rounded-lg bg-soft px-1.5 py-1 outline-none">
                  <option value={2}>H2</option><option value={3}>H3</option>
                </select>
              )}
              <button onClick={() => move(i, -1)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-muted hover:bg-soft"><ArrowUp size={14} /></button>
              <button onClick={() => move(i, 1)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-muted hover:bg-soft"><ArrowDown size={14} /></button>
              <button onClick={() => remove(i)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50"><Trash2 size={14} /></button>
            </div>

            {/* Block body */}
            {b.type === 'heading' && (
              <input value={b.text} onChange={e => update(i, { text: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="Heading text..." className={`${inputCls} font-bold`} />
            )}
            {(b.type === 'paragraph' || b.type === 'quote') && (
              <textarea value={b.text} onChange={e => update(i, { text: e.target.value })}
                placeholder={b.type === 'quote' ? 'Quote text...' : 'Write something...'}
                rows={3} className={inputCls} />
            )}
            {b.type === 'list' && (
              <>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-body mb-2">
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={!!b.ordered} onChange={e => update(i, { ordered: e.target.checked })} />
                  Numbered list
                </label>
                <textarea value={(b.items || []).join('\n')}
                  onChange={e => update(i, { items: e.target.value.split('\n') })}
                  placeholder="One item per line..." rows={3} className={inputCls} />
              </>
            )}
            {b.type === 'image' && (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {b.url && <img src={b.url} alt="" className="rounded-xl w-full max-h-48 object-cover" />}
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input ref={el => (fileRefs.current[i] = el)} type="file" accept="image/*" hidden
                    onChange={e => handleImage(i, e.target.files?.[0])} />
                  <button onClick={() => fileRefs.current[i]?.click()}
                    className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-brand-soft text-brand text-xs font-semibold">
                    <Upload size={13} /> Upload
                  </button>
                  <input value={b.url} onChange={e => update(i, { url: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="...or paste image URL" className={`${inputCls} flex-1`} />
                </div>
                <input value={b.caption || ''} onChange={e => update(i, { caption: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="Caption (optional)" className={inputCls} />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
        {!blocks.length && (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted text-center py-8 border border-dashed border-line rounded-2xl">
            Add your first block from the toolbar above 👆
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/pages/write/ArticleEditor.jsx ====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate, useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  Save, Send, Globe, Eye, X, Calendar, ChevronLeft, ImagePlus, Trash2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { fetchCategories } from '../../services/blogService'
import {
  fetchPostForEdit, uploadTo, savePost, slugify,
} from '../../services/articleService'
import BlockEditor from '../../components/blog/BlockEditor.jsx'
import BlockRenderer from '../../components/blog/BlockRenderer.jsx'
import useAuthorRole from '../../hooks/useAuthorRole.js'

const STATUS_STYLE = {
  draft: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600', review: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700',
  scheduled: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700', published: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700',
  rejected: 'bg-red-100 text-red-700', archived: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400',
}

export default function ArticleEditor() {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const nav = useNavigate()
  const { isStaff, loading: roleLoading } = useAuthorRole()

  const [cats, setCats] = useState([])
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    title: '', subtitle: '', excerpt: '', category_id: '',
    cover_image_url: '', seo_title: '', meta_description: '',
  })
  const [blocks, setBlocks] = useState([])
  const [slug, setSlug] = useState('')
  const [status, setStatusState] = useState('draft')
  const [rejection, setRejection] = useState('')
  const [scheduleAt, setScheduleAt] = useState('')
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState(null)
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState(false)
  const [deny, setDeny] = useState(false)
  const [coverUp, setCoverUp] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => { fetchCategories().then(setCats).catch(console.error) }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!id) return
    fetchPostForEdit(id).then(p => {
      setForm({
        title: p.title || '', subtitle: p.subtitle || '', excerpt: p.excerpt || '',
        category_id: p.category_id || '', cover_image_url: p.cover_image_url || '',
        seo_title: p.seo_title || '', meta_description: p.meta_description || '',
      })
      setBlocks(p.content || [])
      setSlug(p.slug || '')
      setStatusState(p.status)
      setRejection(p.rejection_reason || '')
    }).catch(() => setDeny(true))
  }, [id])

  const words = blocks.map(b => b.text || (b.items || []).join(' ')).join(' ')
    .split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length
  const readMins = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(words / 200))
  const set = (k, v) => setForm(f => ({ ...f, [k]: v }))
  const inputCls = 'w-full bg-soft rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand/20'

  async function persist(overrides = {}) {
    if (!form.title.trim()) { setMsg({ ok: false, text: 'Title is required' }); return null }
    setSaving(true); setMsg(null)
    const payload = {
      title: form.title.trim(),
      subtitle: form.subtitle || null,
      excerpt: form.excerpt || null,
      category_id: form.category_id || null,
      cover_image_url: form.cover_image_url || null,
      seo_title: form.seo_title || null,
      meta_description: form.meta_description || null,
      content: blocks.map(({ id: _b, ...rest }) => rest),
      slug: slug || slugify(form.title) || 'article',
      status,
      ...overrides,
    }
    try {
      let p = null
      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3 && !p; attempt++) {
        try { p = await savePost(payload, id || null) }
        catch (e) {
          if (e.code === '23505') { payload.slug = `${payload.slug}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)}`; continue }
          throw e
        }
      }
      if (!id) nav(`/write/${p.id}`, { replace: true })
      setSlug(p.slug)
      setStatusState(p.status)
      return p
    } catch (e) {
      setMsg({ ok: false, text: e.message })
      return null
    } finally { setSaving(false) }
  }

  const saveDraft = async () => { if (await persist({ status: 'draft' })) setMsg({ ok: true, text: 'Draft saved ✓' }) }
  const submitReview = async () => { if (await persist({ status: 'review' })) setMsg({ ok: true, text: 'Submitted for review ✓' }) }
  const publish = async () => {
    if (await persist({ status: 'published', published_at: new Date().toISOString() }))
      setMsg({ ok: true, text: 'Published live 🎉' })
  }
  const schedule = async () => {
    if (!scheduleAt) { setMsg({ ok: false, text: 'Pick a date & time first' }); return }
    if (await persist({ status: 'scheduled', scheduled_at: new Date(scheduleAt).toISOString() }))
      setMsg({ ok: true, text: 'Scheduled ✓ (auto-publishes if pg_cron enabled)' })
  }

  async function handleCover(e) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
    if (!file) return
    setCoverUp(true)
    try { set('cover_image_url', await uploadTo('blog-covers', file)) }
    catch (err) { setMsg({ ok: false, text: 'Upload failed: ' + err.message }) }
    finally { setCoverUp(false) }
  }

  if (deny) return (
    <div className="min-h-[60vh] grid place-items-center text-body text-sm">
      You don't have access to this article. <Link to="/my-articles" className="ml-2 text-brand underline">My Articles</Link>
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto">
      {/* Top bar */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-5">
        <Link to="/my-articles" className="p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-soft text-body"><ChevronLeft size={18} /></Link>
        <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-ink">{id ? 'Edit Article' : 'Write a Story'}</h1>
        <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full ${STATUS_STYLE[status]}`}>{status}</span>
      </div>

      {rejection && status === 'rejected' && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-4 rounded-2xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-sm text-red-700">
          <b>Rejected by editor:</b> {rejection}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_300px] gap-6 items-start">
        {/* MAIN */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="bg-card border border-line rounded-3xl p-5 md:p-7 space-y-3">
            <input value={form.title} onChange={e => set('title', e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Article title..." className="w-full text-2xl font-extrabold text-ink bg-transparent outline-none placeholder:text-muted/60" />
            <input value={form.subtitle} onChange={e => set('subtitle', e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Subtitle (optional)" className={inputCls} />
            <textarea value={form.excerpt} onChange={e => set('excerpt', e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Short excerpt shown on cards..." rows={2} className={inputCls} />
            <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <select value={form.category_id} onChange={e => set('category_id', e.target.value)} className={inputCls}>
                <option value="">Select category...</option>
                {cats.map(c => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>)}
              </select>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted self-center">⏱ Auto reading time: <b>{readMins} min</b> (calculated on save)</p>
            </div>

            {/* Cover */}
            <div>
              {form.cover_image_url
                ? <div className="relative">
                    <img src={form.cover_image_url} alt="" className="rounded-2xl w-full aspect-video object-cover" />
                    <button onClick={() => set('cover_image_url', '')}
                      className="absolute top-2 right-2 p-2 rounded-xl bg-black/50 text-white"><Trash2 size={15} /></button>
                  </div>
                : <label className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 border-2 border-dashed border-line rounded-2xl py-10 cursor-pointer hover:border-brand hover:bg-brand-soft/40 transition">
                    {coverUp ? <span className="text-sm text-muted animate-pulse">Uploading...</span> : (
                      <><ImagePlus size={26} className="text-muted" />
                        <span className="text-sm text-muted">Upload cover image</span></>
                    )}
                    <input type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onChange={handleCover} />
                  </label>}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* CONTENT BLOCKS */}
          <div className="bg-card border border-line rounded-3xl p-5 md:p-7">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted mb-3">Content</p>
            <BlockEditor blocks={blocks} onChange={setBlocks} />
          </div>

          {/* SEO */}
          <div className="bg-card border border-line rounded-3xl p-5 md:p-7 space-y-3">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">SEO (optional)</p>
            <input value={form.seo_title} onChange={e => set('seo_title', e.target.value)}
              placeholder="SEO title (defaults to article title)" className={inputCls} />
            <textarea value={form.meta_description} onChange={e => set('meta_description', e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Meta description for Google..." rows={2} className={inputCls} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* PUBLISH PANEL */}
        <aside className="lg:sticky lg:top-24 space-y-4">
          <div className="bg-card border border-line rounded-3xl p-5 space-y-2.5">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted mb-1">Publish</p>
            <button onClick={saveDraft} disabled={saving}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-soft text-body text-sm font-bold hover:bg-slate-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition">
              <Save size={15} /> Save Draft
            </button>
            <button onClick={submitReview} disabled={saving}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-brand text-white text-sm font-bold hover:bg-brand-dark disabled:opacity-50 transition">
              <Send size={15} /> Submit for Review
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => setPreview(true)}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-line text-body text-sm font-bold hover:border-brand transition">
              <Eye size={15} /> Preview
            </button>

            {isStaff && !roleLoading && (
              <>
                <div className="border-t border-line my-2" />
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-brand">Staff actions</p>
                <button onClick={publish} disabled={saving}
                  className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-cta text-white text-sm font-bold hover:bg-cta-dark glow disabled:opacity-50 transition">
                  <Globe size={15} /> Publish Now
                </button>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input type="datetime-local" value={scheduleAt} onChange={e => setScheduleAt(e.target.value)}
                    className="flex-1 text-xs bg-soft rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 outline-none text-ink" />
                  <button onClick={schedule} disabled={saving} title="Schedule"
                    className="px-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 text-sm font-bold hover:bg-blue-200 transition">
                    <Calendar size={15} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-muted leading-relaxed px-1">
            Flow: Draft → Review → Published. Community submissions are approved by editors;
            staff can publish directly.
          </p>
        </aside>
      </div>

      {/* PREVIEW MODAL */}
      {preview && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/60 overflow-y-auto p-4" onClick={() => setPreview(false)}>
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto bg-card rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-10 my-6" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <button onClick={() => setPreview(false)}
              className="mb-4 p-2 rounded-xl bg-soft text-body hover:bg-slate-200"><X size={17} /></button>
            {form.category_id && (
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-brand uppercase tracking-wide">
                {cats.find(c => c.id === form.category_id)?.name}
              </p>
            )}
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-ink mt-2">{form.title || 'Untitled'}</h1>
            {form.subtitle && <p className="text-body text-lg mt-1">{form.subtitle}</p>}
            {form.cover_image_url && (
              <img src={form.cover_image_url} alt="" className="rounded-2xl w-full aspect-video object-cover mt-5" />
            )}
            <div className="mt-6"><BlockRenderer blocks={blocks} /></div>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted mt-8 text-center">{readMins} min read · Preview mode</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TOAST */}
      {msg && (
        <div className={`fixed bottom-24 md:bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-sm font-semibold shadow-xl ${msg.ok ? 'bg-brand text-white' : 'bg-red-600 text-white'}`}>
          {msg.text}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

==== FILE: src/pages/MyArticles.jsx ====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { PenLine, Send, Trash2, Eye, FileText, LogIn } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'
import { fetchMyPosts, setPostStatus, deleteMyPost } from '../services/articleService'
import EmptyState from '../components/ui/EmptyState.jsx'

const TABS = [
  { key: 'draft', label: 'Drafts' },
  { key: 'review', label: 'Pending Review' },
  { key: 'published', label: 'Published' },
  { key: 'rejected', label: 'Rejected' },
]
const BADGE = {
  draft: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600', review: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700',
  scheduled: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700', published: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700',
  rejected: 'bg-red-100 text-red-700',
}
const fmt = d => d ? new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }) : ''

export default function MyArticles() {
  const nav = useNavigate()
  const [user, setUser] = useState(undefined)
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState([])
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('draft')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => { supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data }) => setUser(data.user)) }, [])
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return
    setLoading(true)
    fetchMyPosts().then(setPosts).catch(console.error).finally(() => setLoading(false))
  }, [user])

  const list = posts.filter(p => p.status === tab)

  async function handleSubmit(p) {
    await setPostStatus(p.id, 'review')
    setPosts(prev => prev.map(x => x.id === p.id ? { ...x, status: 'review' } : x))
  }
  async function handleDelete(p) {
    if (!confirm(`Delete "${p.title}"?`)) return
    await deleteMyPost(p.id)
    setPosts(prev => prev.filter(x => x.id !== p.id))
  }

  if (user === undefined) return <div className="h-40 animate-pulse bg-card rounded-3xl" />

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-brand-soft text-brand grid place-items-center"><FileText size={19} /></span>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-ink">My Articles</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted">Your drafts, submissions and published stories</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Link to="/write" className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-cta text-white text-sm font-bold glow hover:bg-cta-dark transition">
          <PenLine size={15} /> Write
        </Link>
      </div>

      {!user ? (
        <EmptyState icon={LogIn} title="Log in to manage your articles">
          <Link to="/login" className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-brand text-white text-sm font-bold">Log in</Link>
        </EmptyState>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 mb-5">
            {TABS.map(t => (
              <button key={t.key} onClick={() => setTab(t.key)}
                className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition ${tab === t.key ? 'bg-brand text-white glow' : 'bg-card text-body border border-line hover:border-brand'}`}>
                {t.label} ({posts.filter(p => p.status === t.key).length})
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">{[...Array(3)].map((_, i) => <div key={i} className="h-24 bg-card rounded-2xl animate-pulse" />)}</div>
          ) : list.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState icon={FileText}
              title={`No ${TABS.find(t => t.key === tab)?.label.toLowerCase()} yet`}
              subtitle="Write a travel story and share it with the community.">
              <Link to="/write" className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-brand text-white text-sm font-bold">Write a Story</Link>
            </EmptyState>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {list.map(p => (
                <div key={p.id} className="bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
                  <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-brand-soft overflow-hidden shrink-0">
                    {p.cover_image_url
                      ? <img src={p.cover_image_url} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                      : <div className="w-full h-full grid place-items-center text-xl opacity-40">📝</div>}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-[200px]">
                    <p className="font-bold text-ink text-sm">{p.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted mt-0.5">
                      {p.blog_categories?.name} · {fmt(p.published_at || p.created_at)} · {p.views_count} views
                    </p>
                    {p.status === 'rejected' && p.rejection_reason && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-red-600 mt-1">Reason: {p.rejection_reason}</p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <span className={`text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full ${BADGE[p.status]}`}>{p.status}</span>
                  <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                    {['draft', 'rejected', 'review'].includes(p.status) && (
                      <button onClick={() => nav(`/write/${p.id}`)} title="Edit"
                        className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-soft text-body hover:bg-brand-soft hover:text-brand transition"><PenLine size={15} /></button>
                    )}
                    {p.status === 'draft' && (
                      <button onClick={() => handleSubmit(p)} title="Submit for review"
                        className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-brand-soft text-brand hover:bg-brand hover:text-white transition"><Send size={15} /></button>
                    )}
                    {p.status === 'published' && (
                      <button onClick={() => nav(`/blog/${p.slug}`)} title="View"
                        className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-soft text-body hover:bg-brand-soft hover:text-brand transition"><Eye size={15} /></button>
                    )}
                    {['draft', 'rejected', 'review'].includes(p.status) && (
                      <button onClick={() => handleDelete(p)} title="Delete"
                        className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-red-50 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-100 transition"><Trash2 size={15} /></button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

==== FILE: src/pages/admin/AdminBlog.jsx ====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  FileText, Send, Globe, Archive, Star, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Trash2,
  ShieldAlert, MessageSquare, Users, Eye, LayoutDashboard, Flag,
} from 'lucide-react'
import {
  fetchAdminStats, fetchAllPosts, adminSetStatus, toggleFeatured, adminDeletePost,
  fetchReports, resolveReport, fetchRecentComments, removeComment,
  fetchAuthors, setAuthorType,
} from '../../services/adminService'
import useAuthorRole from '../../hooks/useAuthorRole.js'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState.jsx'

const BADGE = {
  draft: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600', review: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700',
  scheduled: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700', published: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700',
  rejected: 'bg-red-100 text-red-700', archived: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400',
  pending: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700', reviewed: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700',
  dismissed: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500', actioned: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700',
}
const REASONS = {
  spam: 'Spam', fake_info: 'Fake info', copyright: 'Copyright', harassment: 'Harassment',
  unsafe: 'Unsafe', misleading: 'Misleading', other: 'Other',
}
const fmt = d => d ? new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) : ''

const TABS = [
  { key: 'articles', label: 'Articles', icon: FileText },
  { key: 'review', label: 'Review Queue', icon: Send },
  { key: 'reports', label: 'Reports', icon: Flag },
  { key: 'comments', label: 'Comments', icon: MessageSquare },
  { key: 'authors', label: 'Authors', icon: Users },
]

function MiniStat({ icon: Icon, label, value, tint }) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl p-3.5 flex items-center gap-3">
      <span className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl grid place-items-center shrink-0 ${tint}`}><Icon size={17} /></span>
      <div><p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-ink leading-none">{value ?? '—'}</p>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-muted mt-0.5">{label}</p></div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function AdminBlog() {
  const { isStaff, isAdmin, loading: roleLoading } = useAuthorRole()
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('articles')
  const [stats, setStats] = useState(null)
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState([])
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState(null)
  const [reports, setReports] = useState([])
  const [comments, setComments] = useState([])
  const [authors, setAuthors] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  function loadStats() { fetchAdminStats().then(setStats).catch(console.error) }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isStaff) return
    loadStats()
  }, [isStaff])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isStaff) return
    setLoading(true)
    ;(async () => {
      try {
        if (tab === 'articles') setPosts(await fetchAllPosts(filter))
        else if (tab === 'review') setPosts(await fetchAllPosts('review'))
        else if (tab === 'reports') setReports(await fetchReports())
        else if (tab === 'comments') setComments(await fetchRecentComments())
        else if (tab === 'authors') setAuthors(await fetchAuthors())
      } catch (e) { console.error(e) }
      finally { setLoading(false) }
    })()
  }, [tab, filter, isStaff])

  if (roleLoading) return <div className="h-64 animate-pulse bg-card rounded-3xl" />
  if (!isStaff) return (
    <EmptyState icon={ShieldAlert} title="Access denied"
      subtitle="Only editors, staff writers and admins can open this dashboard." />
  )

  async function act(fn, ...args) {
    try { await fn(...args); loadStats(); setPosts(await fetchAllPosts(tab === 'review' ? 'review' : filter)) }
    catch (e) { alert(e.message) }
  }

  const reject = p => {
    const reason = prompt(`Reject "${p.title}" — reason?`)
    if (reason === null) return
    act(adminSetStatus, p.id, 'rejected', { rejection_reason: reason || 'Did not meet guidelines' })
  }

  const FILTERS = [null, 'published', 'draft', 'review', 'scheduled', 'archived', 'rejected']

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <span className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-grad-from to-grad-to text-white grid place-items-center glow">
          <LayoutDashboard size={19} />
        </span>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-ink">Blog Admin</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">Editorial control center — Madventure Magazine</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3">
        <MiniStat icon={FileText} label="Total" value={stats?.total} tint="bg-brand-soft text-brand" />
        <MiniStat icon={Globe} label="Published" value={stats?.published} tint="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600" />
        <MiniStat icon={FileText} label="Drafts" value={stats?.draft} tint="bg-slate-100 text-slate-500" />
        <MiniStat icon={Send} label="Pending" value={stats?.review} tint="bg-amber-50 text-amber-600" />
        <MiniStat icon={Eye} label="Total Views" value={stats?.views?.toLocaleString()} tint="bg-blue-50 text-blue-600" />
        <MiniStat icon={Flag} label="Reports" value={stats?.pendingReports} tint="bg-red-50 text-red-500" />
      </div>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
        {TABS.map(t => (
          <button key={t.key} onClick={() => setTab(t.key)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition ${tab === t.key ? 'bg-brand text-white glow' : 'bg-card text-body border border-line hover:border-brand'}`}>
            <t.icon size={14} /> {t.label}
            {t.key === 'review' && stats?.review > 0 && (
              <span className="min-w-[18px] h-[18px] px-1 rounded-full bg-cta text-white text-[10px] font-bold grid place-items-center">{stats.review}</span>
            )}
            {t.key === 'reports' && stats?.pendingReports > 0 && (
              <span className="min-w-[18px] h-[18px] px-1 rounded-full bg-cta text-white text-[10px] font-bold grid place-items-center">{stats.pendingReports}</span>
            )}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">{[...Array(4)].map((_, i) => <div key={i} className="h-16 bg-card rounded-2xl animate-pulse" />)}</div>
      ) : (

        /* ---------------- ARTICLES ---------------- */
        (tab === 'articles' || tab === 'review') && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {tab === 'articles' && (
              <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
                {FILTERS.map(f => (
                  <button key={f || 'all'} onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
                    className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition ${filter === f ? 'bg-ink text-white' : 'bg-card border border-line text-muted hover:border-brand'}`}>
                    {f || 'All'}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
            {posts.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState icon={FileText} title="Nothing here yet" />
            ) : posts.map(p => (
              <div key={p.id} className="bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-[220px]">
                  <Link to={`/write/${p.id}`} className="font-bold text-ink text-sm hover:text-brand">{p.title}</Link>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted mt-0.5">
                    {p.blog_authors?.display_name || '—'} · {p.blog_categories?.name || 'No category'} · {fmt(p.created_at)} · {p.views_count} views
                  </p>
                  {p.rejection_reason && <p className="text-xs text-red-500 mt-0.5">✕ {p.rejection_reason}</p>}
                </div>
                <span className={`text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full ${BADGE[p.status]}`}>{p.status}</span>
                <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                  <button onClick={() => act(toggleFeatured, p)} title={p.is_featured ? 'Unfeature' : 'Feature'}
                    className={`p-2 rounded-xl transition ${p.is_featured ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-500' : 'bg-soft text-muted hover:text-amber-500'}`}>
                    <Star size={15} fill={p.is_featured ? 'currentColor' : 'none'} />
                  </button>
                  {p.status !== 'published' && (
                    <button onClick={() => act(adminSetStatus, p.id, 'published')} title="Approve & Publish"
                      className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-100 transition"><CheckCircle2 size={15} /></button>
                  )}
                  {p.status === 'published' && (
                    <button onClick={() => act(adminSetStatus, p.id, 'archived')} title="Unpublish / Archive"
                      className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-soft text-body hover:bg-slate-200 transition"><Archive size={15} /></button>
                  )}
                  {p.status !== 'rejected' && p.status !== 'published' && (
                    <button onClick={() => reject(p)} title="Reject"
                      className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-red-50 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-100 transition"><XCircle size={15} /></button>
                  )}
                  {isAdmin && (
                    <button onClick={() => { if (confirm(`DELETE "${p.title}" permanently?`)) act(adminDeletePost, p.id) }}
                      title="Delete (admin)"
                      className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-red-50 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-100 transition"><Trash2 size={15} /></button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )

        /* ---------------- REPORTS ---------------- */
        || (tab === 'reports' && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {reports.length === 0 ? <EmptyState icon={Flag} title="No reports — all clear 🎉" /> : reports.map(r => (
              <div key={r.id} className="bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <span className={`text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full ${BADGE[r.status]}`}>{REASONS[r.reason] || r.reason}</span>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-[200px]">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{r.blog_posts?.title || r.blog_comments?.content?.slice(0, 60) || '—'}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted mt-0.5">{r.details || 'No details'} · {fmt(r.created_at)}</p>
                </div>
                {r.status === 'pending' && (
                  <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                    <button onClick={() => act(resolveReport, r.id, 'dismissed')}
                      className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-soft text-body text-xs font-bold hover:bg-slate-200">Dismiss</button>
                    <button onClick={() => act(resolveReport, r.id, 'actioned')}
                      className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-brand text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-brand-dark">Actioned</button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))

        /* ---------------- COMMENTS ---------------- */
        || (tab === 'comments' && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {comments.length === 0 ? <EmptyState icon={MessageSquare} title="No comments yet" /> : comments.map(c => (
              <div key={c.id} className={`bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3 ${c.status !== 'visible' ? 'opacity-50' : ''}`}>
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-brand-soft text-brand grid place-items-center text-xs font-bold shrink-0">
                  {(c.blog_authors?.display_name || 'U')[0]}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm"><b className="text-ink">{c.blog_authors?.display_name || 'User'}</b>
                    <span className="text-muted text-xs"> on {c.blog_posts?.title || '—'} · {fmt(c.created_at)}</span></p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-body mt-0.5">{c.content}</p>
                </div>
                {c.status === 'visible' && (
                  <button onClick={() => act(removeComment, c.id)}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-red-50 text-red-500 text-xs font-bold hover:bg-red-100 shrink-0">Remove</button>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))

        /* ---------------- AUTHORS ---------------- */
        || (tab === 'authors' && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {authors.map(a => (
              <div key={a.user_id} className="bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-brand-soft text-brand grid place-items-center text-sm font-bold shrink-0">
                  {(a.display_name || 'U')[0]}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-[180px]">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">{a.display_name || 'Traveler'} {a.is_verified && <span className="text-brand">✓</span>}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted">@{a.username || '—'} · {a.articles_count} articles</p>
                </div>
                {isAdmin ? (
                  <select value={a.author_type}
                    onChange={e => { if (confirm(`Change role to ${e.target.value}?`)) act(setAuthorType, a.user_id, e.target.value) }}
                    className="text-xs bg-soft rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 outline-none text-ink">
                    {['admin', 'editor', 'staff_writer', 'verified_traveler', 'community_writer', 'guest_author'].map(t => (
                      <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-soft text-body">{a.author_type}</span>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/components/layout/Sidebar.jsx ====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { NavLink } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ChevronRight, ChevronsLeft, Sparkles, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { NAV } from '../../config/navigation.jsx'
import { useLayout } from '../../context/LayoutContext.jsx'
import useAuthorRole from '../../hooks/useAuthorRole.js'

function NavGroup({ sec, collapsed, open, toggle }) {
  const isOpen = open.has(sec.group)

  if (collapsed) {
    return (
      <div className="relative group">
        <button className="w-full grid place-items-center py-2.5 rounded-xl text-body hover:bg-brand-soft transition">
          <sec.icon size={19} className="text-brand" />
        </button>
        <div className="invisible opacity-0 translate-x-1 group-hover:visible group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover:translate-x-0 transition-all absolute left-full top-0 ml-2 w-52 bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-1.5 z-50">
          <p className="px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">{sec.group}</p>
          {sec.items.map(it => (
            <NavLink key={it.to} to={it.to}
              className={({ isActive }) => `block px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-[13px] font-medium ${isActive ? 'bg-brand-soft text-brand' : 'text-body hover:bg-soft'}`}>
              {it.name}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <button onClick={() => toggle(sec.group)}
        className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold text-body hover:bg-brand-soft hover:text-brand transition">
        <sec.icon size={18} className="text-brand shrink-0" />
        <span className="flex-1 text-left">{sec.group}</span>
        <ChevronRight size={14} className={`transition-transform ${isOpen ? 'rotate-90' : ''}`} />
      </button>
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="mt-0.5 ml-4 pl-4 border-l border-line space-y-0.5">
          {sec.items.map(it => (
            <NavLink key={it.to} to={it.to}
              className={({ isActive }) => `block px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-[13px] transition ${isActive ? 'bg-brand-soft text-brand font-semibold' : 'text-body hover:text-ink hover:bg-soft font-medium'}`}>
              {it.name}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function Sidebar() {
  const { collapsed, setCollapsed, mobileOpen, setMobileOpen } = useLayout()
  const { isStaff } = useAuthorRole()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(() => new Set(['Explore', 'My Madventure']))

  const toggle = g => setOpen(prev => {
    const n = new Set(prev)
    n.has(g) ? n.delete(g) : n.add(g)
    return n
  })

  const nav = NAV.filter(sec => sec.divider || !sec.staffOnly || isStaff)

  return (
    <>
      {mobileOpen && (
        <div onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm md:hidden" />
      )}

      <aside className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 flex flex-col bg-card border-r border-line transition-all duration-300
        ${collapsed ? 'w-[76px]' : 'w-64'}
        ${mobileOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'} md:translate-x-0`}>

        <div className="h-16 flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 border-b border-line shrink-0">
          <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-grad-from to-grad-to grid place-items-center text-white font-extrabold glow shrink-0">M</div>
          {!collapsed && (
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="font-extrabold text-ink leading-tight tracking-tight">MADVENTURE</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-muted font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">Travel OS</p>
            </div>
          )}
          <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} className="ml-auto md:hidden text-muted"><X size={18} /></button>
        </div>

        <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-4 space-y-1">
          {nav.map((sec, i) => sec.divider
            ? <div key={i} className="my-3 border-t border-line" />
            : <NavGroup key={sec.group} sec={sec} collapsed={collapsed} open={open} toggle={toggle} />
          )}
        </nav>

        <div className="p-3 border-t border-line shrink-0">
          {collapsed ? (
            <button title="Go Pro"
              className="w-11 h-11 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cta to-cta-dark text-white grid place-items-center glow hover:scale-105 transition">
              <Sparkles size={18} />
            </button>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-grad-from via-grad-via to-grad-to p-4 text-white relative overflow-hidden">
                <div className="absolute -right-6 -top-6 w-24 h-24 rounded-full hero-glow" />
                <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-white/70">Current plan</p>
                <p className="font-extrabold text-lg leading-tight">Free</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/80 mt-1 leading-snug">Unlock AI Planner, exclusive deals & offline maps.</p>
                <button className="mt-3 w-full py-2 rounded-xl bg-card/95 text-brand-dark text-sm font-bold hover:bg-card transition">
                  Upgrade
                </button>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setCollapsed(true)}
                className="mt-2 w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted hover:text-body py-1.5">
                <ChevronsLeft size={14} /> Collapse
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </aside>

      {collapsed && (
        <button onClick={() => setCollapsed(false)} title="Expand menu"
          className="hidden md:grid fixed left-3 bottom-5 z-30 w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cta to-cta-dark text-white place-items-center glow hover:scale-105 transition">
          <ChevronsLeft size={18} className="rotate-180" />
        </button>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

==== FILE: src/components/layout/Topbar.jsx ====
import { Menu, Search, Bell, PenLine } from 'lucide-react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useLayout } from '../../context/LayoutContext.jsx'
import ThemePicker from '../ui/ThemePicker.jsx'

export default function Topbar() {
  const { setMobileOpen } = useLayout()
  const nav = useNavigate()

  return (
    <header className="h-16 sticky top-0 z-30 bg-card/90 backdrop-blur border-b border-line flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 md:px-6">
      <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)} className="md:hidden p-2 -ml-2 text-body"><Menu size={20} /></button>

      <form onSubmit={e => {
        e.preventDefault()
        const q = new FormData(e.currentTarget).get('q')
        nav(q ? `/blog?search=${encodeURIComponent(q)}` : '/blog')
      }} className="flex-1 max-w-xl relative">
        <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" />
        <input name="q" placeholder="Search Madventure... guides, destinations, hotels"
          className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-soft border border-transparent text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:bg-card focus:border-brand focus:ring-4 focus:ring-brand/10 transition" />
      </form>

      <Link to="/write" title="Write a story"
        className="p-2.5 rounded-xl text-body hover:bg-brand-soft hover:text-brand transition">
        <PenLine size={18} />
      </Link>
      <ThemePicker />
      <Link to="/notifications" className="relative p-2.5 rounded-xl text-body hover:bg-soft transition">
        <Bell size={19} />
        <span className="absolute top-2 right-2 w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-cta ring-2 ring-card" />
      </Link>
      <Link to="/profile" className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-grad-from to-grad-to grid place-items-center text-white text-sm font-bold glow shrink-0">M</Link>
    </header>
  )
}

==== FILE: src/App.jsx ====
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import AppLayout from './components/layout/AppLayout.jsx'
import Dashboard from './pages/Dashboard.jsx'
import Blog from './pages/blog/Blog.jsx'
import BlogArticle from './pages/blog/BlogArticle.jsx'
import Saved from './pages/Saved.jsx'
import Inbox from './pages/Inbox.jsx'
import MyArticles from './pages/MyArticles.jsx'
import ArticleEditor from './pages/write/ArticleEditor.jsx'
import AdminBlog from './pages/admin/AdminBlog.jsx'
import Placeholder from './pages/Placeholder.jsx'

export default function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <Routes>
        <Route element={<AppLayout />}>
          <Route path="/" element={<Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />} />
          <Route path="/dashboard" element={<Dashboard />} />
          <Route path="/blog" element={<Blog />} />
          <Route path="/blog/:slug" element={<BlogArticle />} />
          <Route path="/saved" element={<Saved />} />
          <Route path="/inbox" element={<Inbox />} />
          <Route path="/write" element={<ArticleEditor />} />
          <Route path="/write/:id" element={<ArticleEditor />} />
          <Route path="/my-articles" element={<MyArticles />} />
          <Route path="/admin/blog" element={<AdminBlog />} />
          <Route path="*" element={<Placeholder />} />
        </Route>
      </Routes>
    </BrowserRouter>
  )
}

==== FILE: supabase/schema-editor-patch.sql ====
-- ============================================================
-- EDITOR WORKFLOW PATCH — run in Supabase SQL Editor
-- (fixes: authors can edit + (re)submit their own work)
-- ============================================================

drop policy if exists "posts_update" on public.blog_posts;
create policy "posts_update" on public.blog_posts for update using (
  (author_id = auth.uid() and status in ('draft','rejected','review'))
  or public.is_blog_staff()
) with check (
  (author_id = auth.uid() and status in ('draft','review'))
  or public.is_blog_staff()
);

drop policy if exists "posts_delete" on public.blog_posts;
create policy "posts_delete" on public.blog_posts for delete using (
  (author_id = auth.uid() and status in ('draft','rejected','review'))
  or public.is_blog_admin()
);

-- OPTIONAL: auto-publish scheduled posts every minute
-- (Database → Extensions → enable "pg_cron" first, then uncomment)
-- select cron.schedule('madventure-publish-scheduled', '* * * * *', $$
--   update public.blog_posts
--   set status = 'published', published_at = now()
--   where status = 'scheduled' and scheduled_at <= now()
-- $$);
==== END ====
```

- 🎨 **Sidebar** → Image 1-এর মতো: collapsible, grouped menu (Explore/Travel/Community...), নিচে gradient promo card
- 💬 **Inbox/Chat** → Image 2 & 3-এর মতো: green gradient header, rounded bubbles, avatar, unread badge, orange send button
- 🏠 **Dashboard** → Section 49 vision অনুযায়ী (real DB stats, no fake numbers)
- 🌿 সব জায়গায় আপনার **green color system** (#1B5E20) + orange CTA

```text
==== FILE: src/context/LayoutContext.jsx ====
import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react'

const Ctx = createContext(null)
export const useLayout = () => useContext(Ctx)

export function LayoutProvider({ children }) {
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem('sb_collapsed') === '1')
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem('sb_collapsed', collapsed ? '1' : '0') }, [collapsed])

  return (
    <Ctx.Provider value={{ collapsed, setCollapsed, mobileOpen, setMobileOpen }}>
      {children}
    </Ctx.Provider>
  )
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/components/layout/Sidebar.jsx ====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { NavLink } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ChevronRight, ChevronsLeft, Sparkles, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { NAV } from '../../config/navigation.jsx'
import { useLayout } from '../../context/LayoutContext.jsx'

function NavGroup({ sec, collapsed, open, toggle }) {
  const isOpen = open.has(sec.group)

  if (collapsed) {
    return (
      <div className="relative group">
        <button className="w-full grid place-items-center py-2.5 rounded-xl text-slate-600 hover:bg-brand-soft transition">
          <sec.icon size={19} className="text-brand-light" />
        </button>
        <div className="invisible opacity-0 translate-x-1 group-hover:visible group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover:translate-x-0 transition-all absolute left-full top-0 ml-2 w-52 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-1.5 z-50">
          <p className="px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">{sec.group}</p>
          {sec.items.map(it => (
            <NavLink key={it.to} to={it.to}
              className={({ isActive }) => `block px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-[13px] font-medium ${isActive ? 'bg-brand-soft text-brand' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50'}`}>
              {it.name}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <button onClick={() => toggle(sec.group)}
        className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:bg-brand-soft hover:text-brand transition">
        <sec.icon size={18} className="text-brand-light shrink-0" />
        <span className="flex-1 text-left">{sec.group}</span>
        <ChevronRight size={14} className={`transition-transform ${isOpen ? 'rotate-90' : ''}`} />
      </button>
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="mt-0.5 ml-4 pl-4 border-l border-slate-100 space-y-0.5">
          {sec.items.map(it => (
            <NavLink key={it.to} to={it.to}
              className={({ isActive }) => `block px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-[13px] transition ${isActive ? 'bg-brand-soft text-brand font-semibold' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 font-medium'}`}>
              {it.name}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function Sidebar() {
  const { collapsed, setCollapsed, mobileOpen, setMobileOpen } = useLayout()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(() => new Set(['Explore', 'My Madventure']))

  const toggle = g => setOpen(prev => {
    const n = new Set(prev)
    n.has(g) ? n.delete(g) : n.add(g)
    return n
  })

  return (
    <>
      {mobileOpen && (
        <div onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm md:hidden" />
      )}

      <aside className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 flex flex-col bg-white border-r border-slate-200 transition-all duration-300
        ${collapsed ? 'w-[76px]' : 'w-64'}
        ${mobileOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'} md:translate-x-0`}>

        {/* Logo */}
        <div className="h-16 flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 border-b border-slate-100 shrink-0">
          <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-brand to-brand-light grid place-items-center text-white font-extrabold shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/20 shrink-0">M</div>
          {!collapsed && (
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 leading-tight tracking-tight">MADVENTURE</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">Travel OS</p>
            </div>
          )}
          <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} className="ml-auto md:hidden text-slate-400"><X size={18} /></button>
        </div>

        {/* Menu */}
        <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-4 space-y-1">
          {NAV.map((sec, i) => sec.divider
            ? <div key={i} className="my-3 border-t border-slate-100" />
            : <NavGroup key={sec.group} sec={sec} collapsed={collapsed} open={open} toggle={toggle} />
          )}
        </nav>

        {/* Bottom promo card */}
        <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-100 shrink-0">
          {collapsed ? (
            <button title="Go Pro"
              className="w-11 h-11 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cta to-cta-dark text-white grid place-items-center shadow-lg shadow-orange-500/30 hover:scale-105 transition">
              <Sparkles size={18} />
            </button>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-brand to-emerald-800 p-4 text-white relative overflow-hidden">
                <div className="absolute -right-6 -top-6 w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-white/10" />
                <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-200">Current plan</p>
                <p className="font-extrabold text-lg leading-tight">Free</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-emerald-100 mt-1 leading-snug">Unlock AI Planner, exclusive deals & offline maps.</p>
                <button className="mt-3 w-full py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/95 text-brand text-sm font-bold hover:bg-white transition">
                  Upgrade
                </button>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setCollapsed(true)}
                className="mt-2 w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 py-1.5">
                <ChevronsLeft size={14} /> Collapse
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </aside>

      {collapsed && (
        <button onClick={() => setCollapsed(false)} title="Expand menu"
          className="hidden md:grid fixed left-3 bottom-5 z-30 w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cta to-cta-dark text-white place-items-center shadow-lg shadow-orange-500/30 hover:scale-105 transition">
          <ChevronsLeft size={18} className="rotate-180" />
        </button>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

==== FILE: src/components/layout/Topbar.jsx ====
import { Menu, Search, Bell } from 'lucide-react'
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useLayout } from '../../context/LayoutContext.jsx'

export default function Topbar() {
  const { setMobileOpen } = useLayout()
  const nav = useNavigate()
  const [, setParams] = useSearchParams()

  return (
    <header className="h-16 sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center gap-2 px-4 md:px-6">
      <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)} className="md:hidden p-2 -ml-2 text-slate-600"><Menu size={20} /></button>

      <form onSubmit={e => {
        e.preventDefault()
        const q = new FormData(e.currentTarget).get('q')
        nav(q ? `/blog?search=${encodeURIComponent(q)}` : '/blog')
      }} className="flex-1 max-w-xl relative">
        <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
        <input name="q" placeholder="Search Madventure... guides, destinations, hotels"
          className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100/80 border border-transparent text-sm outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-brand-light focus:ring-4 focus:ring-emerald-500/10 transition" />
      </form>

      <button onClick={() => setParams({})} className="hidden" />
      <Link to="/notifications" className="relative p-2.5 rounded-xl text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 transition">
        <Bell size={19} />
        <span className="absolute top-2 right-2 w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-cta ring-2 ring-white" />
      </Link>
      <Link to="/profile" className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-brand to-brand-light grid place-items-center text-white text-sm font-bold shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/20">M</Link>
    </header>
  )
}

function Shell() {

    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className={`transition-all duration-300 ${collapsed ? 'md:pl-[76px]' : 'md:pl-64'}`}>

          <Outlet />
        </main>
      </div>

    </div>
  )
}

export default function AppLayout() {
  return (
    <LayoutProvider>
      <Shell />
    </LayoutProvider>
  )
}

import { NavLink } from 'react-router-dom'

export default function StatCard({ icon: Icon, label, value, tint = 'bg-brand-soft text-brand', to }) {
  const body = (
    <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3.5 h-full hover:shadow-md hover:border-emerald-200 transition-all">
      <div className={`w-11 h-11 rounded-xl grid place-items-center shrink-0 ${tint}`}><Icon size={20} /></div>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 leading-none">{value ?? '—'}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium mt-1 truncate">{label}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
  return to ? <NavLink to={to} className="block">{body}</NavLink> : body
}

      </div>
```
