```text
==== FILE: src/services/blogService.js ====
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'

const POST_SELECT = `
  id, title, subtitle, slug, excerpt, cover_image_url, reading_time,
  views_count, published_at, updated_at, verify_status, last_verified_at,
  is_featured, destination_id,
  author_id,
  blog_authors!inner(display_name, username, avatar_url, is_verified),
  blog_categories(name, slug)`

export async function fetchPosts({ categorySlug, search, limit = 12 } = {}) {
  let q = supabase
    .from('blog_posts')
    .select(POST_SELECT)
    .eq('status', 'published')
    .order('published_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(limit)

  if (categorySlug) q = q.eq('blog_categories.slug', categorySlug)
  if (search) q = q.or(`title.ilike.%${search}%,excerpt.ilike.%${search}%`)
  const { data, error } = await q
  if (error) throw error
  return data || []
}

export async function fetchFeaturedPost() {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('blog_posts')
    .select(POST_SELECT)
    .eq('status', 'published')
    .eq('is_featured', true)
    .order('featured_order', { ascending: true })
    .limit(1)
    .maybeSingle()
  if (error) throw error
  return data
}

export async function fetchPostBySlug(slug) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('blog_posts')
    .select(`*,
      blog_authors(display_name, username, avatar_url, bio, is_verified),
      blog_categories(name, slug)`)
    .eq('slug', slug)
    .eq('status', 'published')
    .single()
  if (error) throw error
  return data
}

export async function fetchRelatedPosts(postId) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('get_related_posts', { p_post_id: postId, p_limit: 3 })
  if (error) throw error
  return data || []
}

export async function fetchCategories() {
  const { data } = await supabase.from('blog_categories').select('*').order('sort_order')
  return data || []
}

export async function fetchSavedPosts() {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) return null
  const { data } = await supabase.from('blog_post_saves')
    .select(`created_at, blog_posts(id, title, slug, excerpt, cover_image_url, reading_time,
      blog_categories(name, slug), blog_authors(display_name, avatar_url))`)
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
  return (data || []).map(r => r.blog_posts).filter(Boolean)
}

export function recordView(postId) {
  const key = `viewed_${postId}`
  if (sessionStorage.getItem(key)) return
  sessionStorage.setItem(key, '1')
  const sessionId = localStorage.getItem('mv_session') ||
    (() => { const s = crypto.randomUUID(); localStorage.setItem('mv_session', s); return s })()
  supabase.rpc('record_view', { p_post_id: postId, p_session_id: sessionId })
}

export async function toggleSave(postId) {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) throw new Error('login-required')
  const existing = await supabase.from('blog_post_saves')
    .select('post_id').eq('post_id', postId).eq('user_id', user.id).maybeSingle()
  if (existing.data) {
    await supabase.from('blog_post_saves').delete().eq('post_id', postId).eq('user_id', user.id)
    return false
  }
  await supabase.from('blog_post_saves').insert({ post_id: postId })
  return true
}

export async function isSaved(postId) {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) return false
  const { data } = await supabase.from('blog_post_saves')
    .select('post_id').eq('post_id', postId).eq('user_id', user.id).maybeSingle()
  return !!data
}

export async function getMyReaction(postId) {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) return null
  const { data } = await supabase.from('blog_post_reactions')
    .select('type').eq('post_id', postId).eq('user_id', user.id).maybeSingle()
  return data?.type || null
}

export async function toggleReaction(postId, type) {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) throw new Error('login-required')
  const current = await getMyReaction(postId)
  if (current === type) {
    await supabase.from('blog_post_reactions').delete().eq('post_id', postId).eq('user_id', user.id)
    return null
  }
  await supabase.from('blog_post_reactions').upsert({ post_id: postId, user_id: user.id, type })
  return type
}

export async function getReactionCounts(postId) {
  const { data } = await supabase.from('blog_post_reactions').select('type').eq('post_id', postId)
  return (data || []).reduce((acc, r) => ({ ...acc, [r.type]: (acc[r.type] || 0) + 1 }), {})
}

export async function fetchComments(postId) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('blog_comments')
    .select(`*, blog_authors(display_name, avatar_url)`)
    .eq('post_id', postId).eq('status', 'visible')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
  if (error) throw error
  const top = (data || []).filter(c => !c.parent_id)
  return top.map(c => ({ ...c, replies: (data || []).filter(r => r.parent_id === c.id) }))
}

export async function addComment(postId, content, parentId = null) {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) throw new Error('login-required')
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('blog_comments')
    .insert({ post_id: postId, user_id: user.id, content, parent_id: parentId })
    .select().single()
  if (error) throw error
  return data
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/services/dashboardService.js ====
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'

// Real DB numbers only — unavailable tables return null (UI shows "—")
async function countSafe(table) {
  try {
    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
    if (!user) return null
    const { count, error } = await supabase
      .from(table).select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('user_id', user.id)
    return error ? null : (count ?? 0)
  } catch { return null }
}

export async function fetchDashboardStats() {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) return { trips: null, bookings: null, wallet: null, xp: null, saved: null }
  const [trips, bookings, saved] = await Promise.all([
    countSafe('trips'),
    countSafe('bookings'),
    countSafe('blog_post_saves'),
  ])
  return { trips, bookings, wallet: null, xp: null, saved }
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/services/inboxService.js ====
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'

const me = async () => (await supabase.auth.getUser()).data.user

export async function fetchConversations() {
  const user = await me()
  if (!user) return []
  const { data: mine } = await supabase.from('conversation_participants')
    .select('conversation_id, last_read_at').eq('user_id', user.id)
  if (!mine?.length) return []
  const ids = mine.map(p => p.conversation_id)

  const [{ data: parts }, { data: msgs }] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from('conversation_participants')
      .select('conversation_id, user_id, blog_authors(display_name, avatar_url)')
      .in('conversation_id', ids),
    supabase.from('messages').select('*').in('conversation_id', ids).order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
  ])

  const readMap = Object.fromEntries(mine.map(p => [p.conversation_id, p.last_read_at]))
  return ids.map(id => {
    const others = (parts || []).filter(p => p.conversation_id === id && p.user_id !== user.id)
    const convMsgs = (msgs || []).filter(m => m.conversation_id === id)
    const last = convMsgs[0]
    const unread = convMsgs.filter(m =>
      m.sender_id !== user.id && (!readMap[id] || new Date(m.created_at) > new Date(readMap[id]))
    ).length
    const p0 = others[0]
    return {
      id,
      name: others.length > 1 ? `Group · ${others.length + 1}` : (p0?.blog_authors?.display_name || 'Traveler'),
      avatar: p0?.blog_authors?.avatar_url,
      last: last?.content || 'Say hello 👋',
      lastAt: last?.created_at,
      unread,
    }
  }).sort((a, b) => new Date(b.lastAt || 0) - new Date(a.lastAt || 0))
}

export async function fetchPeople() {
  const user = await me()
  if (!user) return []
  const { data } = await supabase.from('blog_authors')
    .select('user_id, display_name, avatar_url')
    .neq('user_id', user.id).limit(30)
  return data || []
}

export async function openDirect(otherId) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('get_or_create_direct', { p_other: otherId })
  if (error) throw error
  return data
}

export async function fetchMessages(convId) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('messages')
    .select('*, blog_authors(display_name, avatar_url)')
    .eq('conversation_id', convId).order('created_at')
  if (error) throw error
  return data || []
}

export async function sendMessage(convId, content) {
  const user = await me()
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('messages')
    .insert({ conversation_id: convId, sender_id: user.id, content }).select().single()
  if (error) throw error
  return data
}

export function subscribeMessages(convId, onInsert) {
  const ch = supabase.channel(`conv_${convId}`)
    .on('postgres_changes',
      { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'messages', filter: `conversation_id=eq.${convId}` },
      payload => onInsert(payload.new))
    .subscribe()
  return () => supabase.removeChannel(ch)
}
==== END ====

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

            No articles published yet.
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Bookmark, LogIn } from 'lucide-react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { fetchSavedPosts } from '../services/blogService'
import BlogCard from '../components/blog/BlogCard.jsx'
import EmptyState from '../components/ui/EmptyState.jsx'

export default function Saved() {
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState(undefined)

  useEffect(() => { fetchSavedPosts().then(setPosts).catch(() => setPosts([])) }, [])

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
        <span className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-pink-50 text-pink-600 grid place-items-center"><Bookmark size={19} /></span>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Saved</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Articles you bookmarked from the Magazine</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {posts === null ? (
        <EmptyState icon={LogIn} title="Log in to see your saved articles">
          <Link to="/login" className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-brand text-white text-sm font-bold">Log in</Link>
        </EmptyState>
      ) : posts === undefined ? (
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
          {[...Array(3)].map((_, i) => <div key={i} className="h-72 bg-white rounded-2xl animate-pulse" />)}
        </div>
      ) : posts.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={Bookmark} title="Nothing saved yet" subtitle="Tap ☆ Save on any article to keep it here." />

        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
          {posts.map(p => <BlogCard key={p.id} post={p} />)}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

==== FILE: src/pages/Placeholder.jsx ====
import { useLocation, Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { Construction, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react'
import { NAV } from '../config/navigation.jsx'

export default function Placeholder() {
  const { pathname } = useLocation()
  let label = pathname
  for (const s of NAV) {
    if (s.items) {
      const f = s.items.find(i => i.to === pathname)
      if (f) label = `${s.group} → ${f.name}`
    }
  }
  return (
    <div className="min-h-[65vh] grid place-items-center">
      <div className="text-center max-w-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl p-10">
        <div className="w-14 h-14 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-brand-soft text-brand grid place-items-center">
          <Construction size={26} />
        </div>
        <h2 className="mt-4 font-extrabold text-xl text-slate-900">{label}</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">
          This section is planned for the next phase of the unified structure.
          Dashboard, Magazine, Saved & Inbox are already live.
        </p>
        <Link to="/dashboard" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-5 text-sm font-semibold text-brand hover:underline">
          <ArrowLeft size={15} /> Back to Dashboard
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

==== FILE: src/pages/Inbox.jsx ====
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { ArrowLeft, Send, Plus, Search, MessageCircle } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'
import {
  fetchConversations, fetchMessages, sendMessage,
  subscribeMessages, fetchPeople, openDirect,
} from '../services/inboxService'

const SIZES = { 7: 'w-7 h-7', 8: 'w-8 h-8', 9: 'w-9 h-9', 10: 'w-10 h-10' }
const fmtTime = d => new Date(d).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' })

function Avatar({ url, name, size = 8 }) {
  return url
    ? <img src={url} alt="" className={`${SIZES[size]} rounded-full object-cover shrink-0`} />
    : <div className={`${SIZES[size]} rounded-full bg-brand-soft text-brand grid place-items-center text-xs font-bold shrink-0`}>
        {(name || 'T')[0]?.toUpperCase()}
      </div>
}

function ConvItem({ c, activeId, onClick }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick}
      className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-2xl text-left transition ${activeId === c.id ? 'bg-brand-soft' : 'hover:bg-slate-50'}`}>
      <div className="relative">
        <Avatar url={c.avatar} name={c.name} size={10} />
        <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-green-500 ring-2 ring-white" />
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 truncate">{c.name}</p>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 shrink-0">{c.lastAt ? fmtTime(c.lastAt) : ''}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 truncate">{c.last}</p>
          {c.unread > 0 && (
            <span className="shrink-0 min-w-[18px] h-[18px] px-1 rounded-full bg-cta text-white text-[10px] font-bold grid place-items-center">
              {c.unread}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </button>
  )
}

export default function Inbox() {
  const [convs, setConvs] = useState([])
  const [active, setActive] = useState(null)
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([])
  const [text, setText] = useState('')
  const [people, setPeople] = useState([])
  const [showPeople, setShowPeople] = useState(false)
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [me, setMe] = useState(null)
  const endRef = useRef(null)

  useEffect(() => { supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data }) => setMe(data.user)) }, [])
  useEffect(() => { fetchConversations().then(setConvs).catch(console.error) }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return
    fetchMessages(active.id).then(setMessages).catch(console.error)
    const unsub = subscribeMessages(active.id, m =>
      setMessages(prev => prev.some(x => x.id === m.id) ? prev : [...prev, m]))
    return unsub
  }, [active?.id])

  useEffect(() => { endRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' }) }, [messages.length])

  async function startChat(userId) {
    const id = await openDirect(userId)
    setShowPeople(false)
    fetchConversations().then(setConvs)
    setActive({ id, name: people.find(p => p.user_id === userId)?.display_name || 'Traveler' })
  }

  async function send(e) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!text.trim() || !active) return
    const m = await sendMessage(active.id, text.trim())
    setMessages(prev => prev.some(x => x.id === m.id) ? prev : [...prev, m])
    setText('')
  }

  const filtered = convs.filter(c => c.name?.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()))

  return (
    <div className="h-[calc(100dvh-11rem)] md:h-[calc(100dvh-8.5rem)] grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-[340px_1fr] bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
      {/* CONVERSATION LIST */}
      <div className={`flex flex-col border-r border-slate-200 min-h-0 ${active ? 'hidden md:flex' : 'flex'}`}>
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-brand to-brand-light text-white p-5 pb-6 rounded-b-[2rem] shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold">Messages</h1>
            <button onClick={async () => {
              setShowPeople(s => !s)
              if (!people.length) fetchPeople().then(setPeople)
            }} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-white/20 grid place-items-center hover:bg-white/30 transition">
              <Plus size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="relative mt-3">
            <Search size={15} className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
            <input value={query} onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search"
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-full text-sm text-slate-700 bg-white outline-none" />
          </div>
        </div>

        {showPeople && (
          <div className="border-b border-slate-100 p-2 max-h-44 overflow-y-auto bg-brand-soft/40 shrink-0">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 px-2 py-1">Start a chat</p>
            {people.map(p => (
              <button key={p.user_id} onClick={() => startChat(p.user_id)}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-2 py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-white text-left transition">
                <Avatar url={p.avatar_url} name={p.display_name} size={8} />
                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 truncate">{p.display_name || 'Traveler'}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
            {!people.length && <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 px-2 py-2">No other users found yet.</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2 min-h-0">
          {filtered.map(c => <ConvItem key={c.id} c={c} activeId={active?.id} onClick={() => setActive(c)} />)}
          {!filtered.length && (
            <div className="text-center py-16 px-6 text-slate-400">
              <MessageCircle size={36} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-40" />
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">No conversations yet</p>
              <p className="text-xs mt-1">Tap + to start chatting with fellow travelers.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CHAT THREAD */}
      <div className={`flex flex-col min-h-0 ${active ? 'flex' : 'hidden md:flex'}`}>
        {active ? (
          <>
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-brand to-brand-light text-white px-4 py-3 flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
              <button onClick={() => setActive(null)} className="md:hidden p-1"><ArrowLeft size={20} /></button>
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-white/25 grid place-items-center font-bold text-sm">
                {(active.name || 'T')[0]?.toUpperCase()}
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="font-bold text-sm leading-tight">{active.name}</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-emerald-100 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-green-300 inline-block" /> Online
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50 p-4 space-y-3 min-h-0">
              {messages.map(m => {
                const mine = m.sender_id === me?.id
                return (
                  <div key={m.id} className={`flex items-end gap-2 ${mine ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}>
                    {!mine && <Avatar url={m.blog_authors?.avatar_url} name={m.blog_authors?.display_name} size={7} />}
                    <div className={`max-w-[75%] px-4 py-2.5 text-sm shadow-sm ${mine
                      ? 'bg-gradient-to-br from-brand to-brand-light text-white rounded-2xl rounded-br-md'
                      : 'bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-2xl rounded-bl-md'}`}>
                      <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{m.content}</p>
                      <p className={`text-[10px] mt-1 text-right ${mine ? 'text-emerald-100' : 'text-slate-400'}`}>
                        {fmtTime(m.created_at)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )
              })}
              <div ref={endRef} />
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={send} className="p-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
              <button type="button" className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 grid place-items-center hover:bg-slate-200 shrink-0">
                <Plus size={18} />
              </button>
              <input value={text} onChange={e => setText(e.target.value)} placeholder="Type your message..."
                className="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand/20" />
              <button className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-cta to-cta-dark text-white grid place-items-center shadow-lg shadow-orange-500/30 hover:scale-105 transition shrink-0">
                <Send size={17} />
              </button>
            </form>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="hidden md:grid place-items-center flex-1 text-slate-400">
            <div className="text-center">
              <MessageCircle size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
              <p className="font-medium">Select a conversation</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/pages/blog/Blog.jsx ====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { Search } from 'lucide-react'
import { fetchCategories, fetchFeaturedPost, fetchPosts } from '../../services/blogService'
import BlogCard from '../../components/blog/BlogCard.jsx'

export default function Blog() {
  const nav = useNavigate()
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams()
  const search = params.get('search') || ''

  const [categories, setCategories] = useState([])
  const [featured, setFeatured] = useState(null)
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState([])
  const [activeCat, setActiveCat] = useState(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => { fetchCategories().then(setCategories).catch(console.error) }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    setLoading(true)
    Promise.all([fetchFeaturedPost(), fetchPosts({ categorySlug: activeCat, search })])
      .then(([f, p]) => { setFeatured(f); setPosts(p.filter(x => x.id !== f?.id)) })
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(() => setLoading(false))
  }, [activeCat, search])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8 -mt-6">
      {/* HERO */}
      <header className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-brand via-brand-light to-emerald-700 text-white p-6 md:p-12 text-center relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute -left-10 -bottom-16 w-48 h-48 rounded-full bg-white/10" />
        <div className="absolute -right-8 -top-12 w-40 h-40 rounded-full bg-white/10" />
        <p className="text-emerald-200 font-semibold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase text-xs">Madventure Magazine</p>
        <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-extrabold mt-2 tracking-tight">Discover places. Plan better journeys.</h1>
        <div className="relative max-w-xl mx-auto mt-6">
          <Search size={17} className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            value={search} onChange={e => setParams(e.target.value ? { search: e.target.value } : {})}
            placeholder="Search travel guides, stories and tips..."
            className="w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-slate-800 bg-white shadow-xl outline-none focus:ring-4 ring-orange-400/30" />
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* CATEGORY CHIPS */}
      <nav className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 -mx-1 px-1">
        <button onClick={() => setActiveCat(null)}
          className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition ${!activeCat ? 'bg-brand text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/20' : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border border-slate-200 hover:border-brand-light'}`}>
          All
        </button>
        {categories.map(c => (
          <button key={c.slug} onClick={() => setActiveCat(activeCat === c.slug ? null : c.slug)}
            className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition ${activeCat === c.slug ? 'bg-brand text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/20' : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border border-slate-200 hover:border-brand-light'}`}>
            {c.name}
          </button>
        ))}
      </nav>

      {/* FEATURED */}
      {featured && !activeCat && !search && (
        <section onClick={() => nav(`/blog/${featured.slug}`)}
          className="grid md:grid-cols-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl overflow-hidden cursor-pointer hover:shadow-xl transition-shadow">
          <div className="aspect-video md:aspect-auto md:h-full bg-brand-soft min-h-[260px]">
            {featured.cover_image_url && <img src={featured.cover_image_url} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />}
          </div>
          <div className="p-8 flex flex-col justify-center">
            <span className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-1 bg-orange-100 text-cta-dark text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">★ FEATURED</span>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-3">{featured.title}</h2>
            <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2 line-clamp-3">{featured.excerpt}</p>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-4 text-sm text-slate-400">
              <span>{featured.blog_authors?.display_name}</span>•<span>{featured.reading_time} min read</span>
            </div>
            <span className="mt-5 w-fit px-5 py-2 bg-cta text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-lg shadow-orange-500/30">Read Article</span>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* GRID */}
      <section>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mb-5">
          {search ? `Results for "${search}"` : activeCat ? categories.find(c => c.slug === activeCat)?.name : 'Latest Stories'}
        </h2>
        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
            {[...Array(6)].map((_, i) => <div key={i} className="h-72 bg-white rounded-2xl animate-pulse" />)}
          </div>
        ) : posts.length === 0 && !featured ? (
          <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400">
            <div className="text-5xl mb-3">📰</div>
            <p className="font-medium">No articles published yet.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
            {posts.map(p => <BlogCard key={p.id} post={p} />)}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: src/pages/blog/BlogArticle.jsx ====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase'
import {
  fetchPostBySlug, fetchRelatedPosts, recordView, toggleSave, isSaved,
  toggleReaction, getMyReaction, getReactionCounts, fetchComments, addComment,
} from '../../services/blogService'

function Block({ b }) {
  switch (b.type) {
    case 'heading': {
      const Tag = `h${b.level || 2}`
      return <Tag id={slugify(b.text)} className={`${b.level === 2 ? 'text-2xl' : 'text-xl'} font-bold text-slate-900 mt-8 mb-3 scroll-mt-24`}>{b.text}</Tag>
    }
    case 'paragraph': return <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed mb-4">{b.text}</p>
    case 'quote': return <blockquote className="border-l-4 border-cta bg-orange-50 pl-4 pr-4 py-3 italic text-slate-700 my-5 rounded-r-xl">{b.text}</blockquote>
    case 'list': {
      const List = b.ordered ? 'ol' : 'ul'
      return <List className={`${b.ordered ? 'list-decimal' : 'list-disc'} pl-6 text-slate-600 space-y-1.5 mb-4`}>
        {(b.items || []).map((it, i) => <li key={i}>{it}</li>)}
      </List>
    }
    case 'image': return (
      <figure className="my-6">
        <img src={b.url} alt={b.caption || ''} className="rounded-2xl w-full" />
        {b.caption && <figcaption className="text-sm text-slate-400 text-center mt-2">{b.caption}</figcaption>}
      </figure>
    )
    default: return null
  }
}

const slugify = t => t.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\u0980-\u09FF]+/g, '-').replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '')
const fmtDate = d => d ? new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }) : ''

export default function BlogArticle() {
  const { slug } = useParams()
  const nav = useNavigate()
  const [post, setPost] = useState(null)
  const [related, setRelated] = useState([])
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false)
  const [reaction, setReaction] = useState(null)
  const [counts, setCounts] = useState({})
  const [comments, setComments] = useState([])
  const [commentText, setCommentText] = useState('')
  const [err, setErr] = useState('')
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => { supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data }) => setUser(data.user)) }, [])
  useEffect(() => { window.scrollTo(0, 0) }, [slug])

  useEffect(() => {
    setErr('')
    fetchPostBySlug(slug).then(async p => {
      setPost(p)
      document.title = `${p.seo_title || p.title} | Madventure`
      recordView(p.id)
      setSaved(await isSaved(p.id))
      setReaction(await getMyReaction(p.id))
      setCounts(await getReactionCounts(p.id))
      fetchComments(p.id).then(setComments).catch(console.error)
      fetchRelatedPosts(p.id).then(setRelated).catch(console.error)
    }).catch(() => setErr('Article not found'))
  }, [slug])

  if (err) return <div className="min-h-[60vh] grid place-items-center text-slate-500">{err} · <Link className="ml-2 text-brand underline" to="/blog">Back</Link></div>
  if (!post) return <div className="min-h-[60vh] grid place-items-center"><div className="animate-pulse h-96 w-full max-w-3xl bg-white rounded-3xl" /></div>

  const toc = (post.content || []).filter(b => b.type === 'heading' && (b.level || 2) <= 3)
  const totalReactions = Object.values(counts).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)

  async function handleSave() {
    try { setSaved(await toggleSave(post.id)) } catch { alert('Please log in to save articles') }
  }
  async function handleReact(type) {
    try {
      const r = await toggleReaction(post.id, type)
      setReaction(r); setCounts(await getReactionCounts(post.id))
    } catch { alert('Please log in to react') }
  }
  async function handleShare() {
    const url = window.location.href
    if (navigator.share) { navigator.share({ title: post.title, url }).catch(() => {}) }
    else { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url); alert('Link copied!') }
  }
  async function handleComment(e) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!commentText.trim()) return
    const c = await addComment(post.id, commentText.trim())
    setComments(prev => [{ ...c, blog_authors: { display_name: user.email.split('@')[0] }, replies: [] }, ...prev])
    setCommentText('')
  }

  return (
    <div className="pb-24 md:pb-10 -mt-6">
      {/* HEADER */}
      <header className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-10">
        <nav className="text-sm text-slate-400 flex gap-1.5">
          <Link to="/blog" className="hover:text-brand">Magazine</Link><span>/</span>
          {post.blog_categories && <span className="text-brand font-semibold">{post.blog_categories.name}</span>}
        </nav>
        <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-4 tracking-tight">{post.title}</h1>
        {post.subtitle && <p className="text-slate-500 text-lg mt-2">{post.subtitle}</p>}

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-5">
          <img src={post.blog_authors?.avatar_url} alt="" className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-brand-soft" />
          <div className="text-sm">
            <p className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{post.blog_authors?.display_name || 'Author'} {post.blog_authors?.is_verified && <span title="Verified" className="text-brand">✓</span>}</p>
            <p className="text-slate-400">
              Published {fmtDate(post.published_at)} · Updated {fmtDate(post.updated_at)} · {post.reading_time} min read · {post.views_count} views
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {post.verify_status === 'verified' && (
          <p className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-green-700 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full">
            ✓ Information verified · Last checked {fmtDate(post.last_verified_at)}
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-5">
          <button onClick={handleSave} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold border transition ${saved ? 'bg-brand text-white border-brand' : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:border-brand-light'}`}>
            {saved ? '★ Saved' : '☆ Save'}
          </button>
          <button onClick={handleShare} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:border-brand-light">↗ Share</button>
          <button onClick={() => handleReact('like')} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold border transition ${reaction === 'like' ? 'bg-cta text-white border-cta' : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:border-cta'}`}>
            👍 Like {totalReactions > 0 && `(${totalReactions})`}
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => handleReact('helpful')} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold border transition ${reaction === 'helpful' ? 'bg-brand-light text-white border-brand-light' : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:border-brand-light'}`}>
            💡 Helpful
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => window.print()} className="hidden md:block px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:border-slate-400">🖨 Print</button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {post.cover_image_url && (
        <div className="mt-6">
          <img src={post.cover_image_url} alt={post.title} className="w-full rounded-3xl shadow-md aspect-video object-cover" />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* BODY + TOC */}
      <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_220px] gap-6 items-start">
        <article className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6 md:p-10 order-2 lg:order-1">
          {(post.content || []).map((b, i) => <Block key={i} b={b} />)}

          {related.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-12 pt-8 border-t border-slate-100">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-lg mb-4">Related Articles</h3>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {related.map(r => (
                  <Link key={r.id} to={`/blog/${r.slug}`} className="block p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-brand-soft/50 border border-transparent hover:border-emerald-200 transition">
                    <p className="font-semibold text-slate-800 text-sm">{r.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">{r.reading_time} min read</p>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <section className="mt-12 pt-8 border-t border-slate-100">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-lg mb-4">Comments ({comments.length})</h3>
            {user ? (
              <form onSubmit={handleComment} className="mb-6">
                <textarea value={commentText} onChange={e => setCommentText(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Write a comment..." rows={3}
                  className="w-full border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 ring-brand/20" />
                <button className="mt-2 px-5 py-2 bg-brand text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-brand-light">Post Comment</button>
              </form>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-400 mb-6">Log in to join the discussion.</p>
            )}
            <div className="space-y-5">
              {comments.map(c => (
                <div key={c.id} className="flex gap-3">
                  <img src={c.blog_authors?.avatar_url} alt="" className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-brand-soft shrink-0" />
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <p className="text-sm"><span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{c.blog_authors?.display_name || 'User'}</span> <span className="text-slate-400 text-xs ml-1">{fmtDate(c.created_at)}</span></p>
                    <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm mt-0.5">{c.content}</p>
                    {c.replies.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="mt-3 space-y-3 border-l-2 border-brand-soft pl-4">
                        {c.replies.map(r => (
                          <div key={r.id} className="text-sm">
                            <p><span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">{r.blog_authors?.display_name || 'User'}</span> <span className="text-slate-400 text-xs ml-1">{fmtDate(r.created_at)}</span></p>
                            <p className="text-slate-600">{r.content}</p>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              {comments.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Be the first to comment.</p>}
            </div>
          </section>
        </article>

        {toc.length >= 3 && (
          <aside className="hidden lg:block order-1 lg:order-2">
            <div className="sticky top-24 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-3">On this page</p>
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                {toc.map((h, i) => (
                  <li key={i}>
                    <a href={`#${slugify(h.text)}`} className="text-sm text-slate-500 hover:text-brand leading-snug block">{h.text}</a>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          </aside>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* MOBILE STICKY BAR */}
      <div className="fixed bottom-[4.5rem] inset-x-4 md:hidden bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-3 flex gap-2 z-20">
        <button onClick={handleSave} className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-semibold text-sm ${saved ? 'bg-brand text-white' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700'}`}>Save</button>
        <button onClick={handleShare} className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-semibold text-sm bg-slate-100 text-slate-700">Share</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
==== END ====

==== FILE: supabase/schema.sql ====
-- ============================================================
-- MADVENTURE — BLOG SCHEMA (run first, if not already run)
-- ============================================================
create table public.blog_categories (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  name text not null,
  slug text not null unique,
  description text,
  icon text,
  sort_order int not null default 0,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
insert into public.blog_categories (name, slug, sort_order) values
('Destination Guides','destination-guides',1),
('Travel Tips','travel-tips',2),
('Travel Stories','travel-stories',3),
('Itineraries','itineraries',4),
('Budget Travel','budget-travel',5),
('Food & Culture','food-culture',6),
('Adventure','adventure',7),
('Photography','photography',8),
('Travel Safety','travel-safety',9),
('Transport','transport',10),
('Hotel Guides','hotel-guides',11),
('Travel News','travel-news',12);

create table public.blog_tags (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  name text not null,
  slug text not null unique,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table public.blog_authors (
  user_id uuid primary key references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  username text unique,
  display_name text,
  avatar_url text,
  bio text,
  author_type text not null default 'community_writer'
    check (author_type in ('admin','editor','staff_writer','verified_traveler','community_writer','guest_author')),
  is_verified boolean not null default false,
  articles_count int not null default 0,
  followers_count int not null default 0,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table public.blog_posts (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  author_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  title text not null,
  subtitle text,
  slug text not null unique,
  excerpt text,
  content jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  cover_image_url text,
  category_id uuid references public.blog_categories(id),
  destination_id uuid,
  status text not null default 'draft'
    check (status in ('draft','review','scheduled','published','archived','rejected')),
  visibility text not null default 'public' check (visibility in ('public','unlisted','private')),
  rejection_reason text,
  reviewer_id uuid references auth.users(id),
  reviewed_at timestamptz,
  is_featured boolean not null default false,
  featured_order int,
  published_at timestamptz,
  scheduled_at timestamptz,
  seo_title text,
  meta_description text,
  canonical_url text,
  og_title text,
  og_description text,
  og_image_url text,
  verify_status text not null default 'none'
    check (verify_status in ('none','verified','needs_review','outdated')),
  last_verified_at timestamptz,
  verified_by uuid references auth.users(id),
  reading_time int not null default 0,
  views_count int not null default 0,
  unique_views int not null default 0,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index idx_posts_published on public.blog_posts (status, published_at desc);
create index idx_posts_category on public.blog_posts (category_id);
create index idx_posts_author on public.blog_posts (author_id);
create index idx_posts_featured on public.blog_posts (is_featured, featured_order);

create table public.blog_post_tags (
  post_id uuid references public.blog_posts(id) on delete cascade,
  tag_id uuid references public.blog_tags(id) on delete cascade,
  primary key (post_id, tag_id)
);

create table public.blog_post_media (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  post_id uuid not null references public.blog_posts(id) on delete cascade,
  url text not null,
  type text not null default 'image' check (type in ('image','video','gallery')),
  sort_order int not null default 0
);

create table public.blog_post_reactions (
  post_id uuid references public.blog_posts(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  type text not null check (type in ('like','helpful','love')),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (post_id, user_id)
);

create table public.blog_post_saves (
  post_id uuid references public.blog_posts(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (post_id, user_id)
);

create table public.blog_comments (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  post_id uuid not null references public.blog_posts(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  parent_id uuid references public.blog_comments(id) on delete cascade,
  content text not null,
  status text not null default 'visible' check (status in ('visible','pending','removed')),
  edited_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index idx_comments_post on public.blog_comments (post_id);

create table public.blog_comment_reactions (
  comment_id uuid references public.blog_comments(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  type text not null default 'like',
  unique (comment_id, user_id)
);

create table public.blog_views (
  id bigint generated always as identity primary key,
  post_id uuid not null references public.blog_posts(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  session_id text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index idx_views_dedupe on public.blog_views (post_id, user_id, session_id, created_at);

create table public.blog_analytics (
  id bigint generated always as identity primary key,
  post_id uuid references public.blog_posts(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  event_type text not null check (event_type in
    ('share','save','cta_click','planner_click','hotel_click','ticket_click','tour_click','destination_click')),
  metadata jsonb default '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table public.blog_post_revisions (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  post_id uuid not null references public.blog_posts(id) on delete cascade,
  editor_id uuid references auth.users(id),
  title text,
  content jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table public.blog_reports (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  reporter_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  post_id uuid references public.blog_posts(id) on delete cascade,
  comment_id uuid references public.blog_comments(id) on delete cascade,
  reason text not null check (reason in
    ('spam','fake_info','copyright','harassment','unsafe','misleading','other')),
  details text,
  status text not null default 'pending' check (status in ('pending','reviewed','dismissed','actioned')),
  reviewed_by uuid references auth.users(id),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table public.newsletter_subscribers (
  email text primary key,
  subscribed_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- ---------- FUNCTIONS & TRIGGERS ----------
create or replace function public.is_blog_staff()
returns boolean language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select exists (select 1 from blog_authors
    where user_id = auth.uid() and author_type in ('admin','editor','staff_writer'));
$$;

create or replace function public.is_blog_admin()
returns boolean language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select exists (select 1 from blog_authors where user_id = auth.uid() and author_type = 'admin');
$$;

create or replace function public.set_updated_at()
returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin new.updated_at = now(); return new; end $$;

create trigger trg_posts_updated before update on public.blog_posts
for each row execute function public.set_updated_at();

create or replace function public.calc_reading_time(content jsonb)
returns int language plpgsql immutable as $$
declare words int := 0;
begin
  select coalesce(sum(array_length(string_to_array(coalesce(b->>'text',''), ' '), 1)), 0)
    into words from jsonb_array_elements(content) b where b ? 'text';
  return greatest(1, ceil(words / 200.0))::int;
end $$;

create or replace function public.set_reading_time()
returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin
  if new.content is not null then new.reading_time := public.calc_reading_time(new.content); end if;
  return new;
end $$;

create trigger trg_posts_reading before insert or update of content
on public.blog_posts for each row execute function public.set_reading_time();

create or replace function public.handle_new_blog_author()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
begin
  insert into public.blog_authors (user_id, username, display_name)
  values (
    new.id,
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'username', split_part(new.email,'@',1)) || '_' || substr(new.id::text,1,6),
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'display_name', split_part(new.email,'@',1))
  ) on conflict (user_id) do nothing;
  return new;
end $$;

drop trigger if exists on_auth_user_created on auth.users;
create trigger on_auth_user_created after insert on auth.users
for each row execute function public.handle_new_blog_author();

alter table public.blog_posts add constraint blog_posts_author_fk
  foreign key (author_id) references public.blog_authors(user_id) on delete cascade;
alter table public.blog_comments add constraint blog_comments_author_fk
  foreign key (user_id) references public.blog_authors(user_id) on delete cascade;

create or replace function public.record_view(p_post_id uuid, p_session_id text)
returns void language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare v_user uuid := auth.uid();
begin
  if not exists (
    select 1 from blog_views
    where post_id = p_post_id and created_at > now() - interval '24 hours'
      and ((v_user is not null and user_id = v_user) or (v_user is null and session_id = p_session_id))
  ) then
    insert into blog_views (post_id, user_id, session_id) values (p_post_id, v_user, p_session_id);
    update blog_posts set views_count = views_count + 1, unique_views = unique_views + 1 where id = p_post_id;
  end if;
end $$;

create or replace function public.get_related_posts(p_post_id uuid, p_limit int default 4)
returns setof public.blog_posts language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select p.* from blog_posts p
  where p.id <> p_post_id and p.status = 'published'
  order by
    (case when p.destination_id = (select destination_id from blog_posts where id = p_post_id) then 2 else 0 end)
  + (case when p.category_id = (select category_id from blog_posts where id = p_post_id) then 1 else 0 end)
  + (select count(*) from blog_post_tags pt
     where pt.post_id = p.id and pt.tag_id in (select tag_id from blog_post_tags where post_id = p_post_id))
  desc, p.published_at desc
  limit p_limit;
$$;

-- ---------- RLS ----------
alter table public.blog_categories enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_tags enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_authors enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_posts enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_post_tags enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_post_media enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_post_reactions enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_post_saves enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_comments enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_comment_reactions enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_views enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_analytics enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_post_revisions enable row level security;
alter table public.blog_reports enable row level security;
alter table public.newsletter_subscribers enable row level security;

create policy "categories_public_read" on public.blog_categories for select using (true);
create policy "categories_staff_write" on public.blog_categories for all using (public.is_blog_staff());
create policy "tags_public_read" on public.blog_tags for select using (true);
create policy "tags_staff_write" on public.blog_tags for all using (public.is_blog_staff());
create policy "authors_read" on public.blog_authors for select using (true);
create policy "authors_self_update" on public.blog_authors for update using (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "authors_admin_all" on public.blog_authors for all using (public.is_blog_admin());

create policy "posts_select" on public.blog_posts for select using (
  (status = 'published' and visibility = 'public') or author_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_staff()
);
create policy "posts_insert" on public.blog_posts for insert with check (
  author_id = auth.uid() and (status in ('draft','review') or public.is_blog_staff())
);
create policy "posts_update" on public.blog_posts for update using (
  (author_id = auth.uid() and status in ('draft','rejected')) or public.is_blog_staff()
);
create policy "posts_delete" on public.blog_posts for delete using (
  (author_id = auth.uid() and status in ('draft','rejected')) or public.is_blog_admin()
);

create policy "post_tags_read" on public.blog_post_tags for select using (
  exists (select 1 from blog_posts p where p.id = post_id and (
    (p.status = 'published' and p.visibility = 'public') or p.author_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_staff()))
);
create policy "post_tags_write" on public.blog_post_tags for all
  using (exists (select 1 from blog_posts p where p.id = post_id and (p.author_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_staff())))
  with check (exists (select 1 from blog_posts p where p.id = post_id and (p.author_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_staff())));

create policy "media_all" on public.blog_post_media for all
  using (exists (select 1 from blog_posts p where p.id = post_id and (p.author_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_staff())))
  with check (exists (select 1 from blog_posts p where p.id = post_id and (p.author_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_staff())));

create policy "reactions_read" on public.blog_post_reactions for select using (true);
create policy "reactions_write" on public.blog_post_reactions for all
  using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "saves_read_own" on public.blog_post_saves for select using (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "saves_write_own" on public.blog_post_saves for all
  using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());

create policy "comments_read_visible" on public.blog_comments for select using (
  status = 'visible' or user_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_staff()
);
create policy "comments_insert" on public.blog_comments for insert with check (
  user_id = auth.uid() and status = 'visible'
);
create policy "comments_update_own" on public.blog_comments for update using (
  user_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_staff()
);
create policy "comments_delete_own" on public.blog_comments for delete using (
  user_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_admin()
);

create policy "creactions_read" on public.blog_comment_reactions for select using (true);
create policy "creactions_write" on public.blog_comment_reactions for all
  using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());

create policy "views_insert_anyone" on public.blog_views for insert with check (true);
create policy "analytics_insert_anyone" on public.blog_analytics for insert with check (true);
create policy "analytics_read_staff" on public.blog_analytics for select using (public.is_blog_staff());
create policy "revisions_staff" on public.blog_post_revisions for all
  using (public.is_blog_staff()) with check (public.is_blog_staff());
create policy "reports_insert" on public.blog_reports for insert with check (reporter_id = auth.uid());
create policy "reports_read" on public.blog_reports for select using (
  reporter_id = auth.uid() or public.is_blog_staff()
);
create policy "reports_moderate" on public.blog_reports for update using (public.is_blog_staff());
create policy "newsletter_subscribe" on public.newsletter_subscribers for insert with check (true);
create policy "newsletter_admin_read" on public.newsletter_subscribers for select using (public.is_blog_admin());

-- ---------- STORAGE ----------
insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public) values
('blog-covers', 'blog-covers', true),
('blog-media', 'blog-media', true);

create policy "blog_storage_read" on storage.objects for select
using (bucket_id in ('blog-covers','blog-media'));
create policy "blog_storage_upload" on storage.objects for insert
to authenticated with check (bucket_id in ('blog-covers','blog-media'));
==== END ====

==== FILE: supabase/schema-inbox.sql ====
-- ============================================================
-- MADVENTURE — INBOX / MESSAGING SCHEMA (run after schema.sql)
-- ============================================================
create table public.conversations (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  type text not null default 'direct' check (type in ('direct','group','business','support')),
  title text,
  image_url text,
  created_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table public.conversation_participants (
  conversation_id uuid not null references public.conversations(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid not null references public.blog_authors(user_id) on delete cascade,
  last_read_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (conversation_id, user_id)
);

create table public.messages (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  conversation_id uuid not null references public.conversations(id) on delete cascade,
  sender_id uuid not null references public.blog_authors(user_id) on delete cascade,
  content text,
  type text not null default 'text' check (type in ('text','image','file','trip_share','hotel_share','ticket_share')),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index idx_messages_conv on public.messages (conversation_id, created_at);

-- Get or create a direct conversation between me and another user
create or replace function public.get_or_create_direct(p_other uuid)
returns uuid language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare cid uuid; me uuid := auth.uid();
begin
  select c.id into cid from conversations c
  join conversation_participants a on a.conversation_id = c.id and a.user_id = me
  join conversation_participants b on b.conversation_id = c.id and b.user_id = p_other
  where c.type = 'direct' limit 1;
  if cid is null then
    insert into conversations (type, created_by) values ('direct', me) returning id into cid;
    insert into conversation_participants (conversation_id, user_id) values (cid, me), (cid, p_other);
  end if;
  return cid;
end $$;

-- ---------- RLS ----------
alter table public.conversations enable row level security;
alter table public.conversation_participants enable row level security;
alter table public.messages enable row level security;

create policy "conv_read" on public.conversations for select using (
  exists (select 1 from conversation_participants cp
          where cp.conversation_id = id and cp.user_id = auth.uid())
);
create policy "conv_insert" on public.conversations for insert with check (created_by = auth.uid());

create policy "cp_read" on public.conversation_participants for select using (
  exists (select 1 from conversation_participants me
          where me.conversation_id = conversation_id and me.user_id = auth.uid())
);
create policy "cp_insert" on public.conversation_participants for insert with check (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "cp_update_own" on public.conversation_participants for update using (user_id = auth.uid());

create policy "msg_read" on public.messages for select using (
  exists (select 1 from conversation_participants cp
          where cp.conversation_id = conversation_id and cp.user_id = auth.uid())
);
create policy "msg_insert" on public.messages for insert with check (
  sender_id = auth.uid() and exists (
    select 1 from conversation_participants cp
    where cp.conversation_id = conversation_id and cp.user_id = auth.uid())
);

-- Realtime
alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.messages;
==== END ====
```

ঠিক আছে! 👍 Plan টা এরকম:

> ⚠️ **সত্যি কথা আগে:** আমি chat থেকে সরাসরি ZIP/file download দিতে পারি না। কিন্তু **smart solution** দিচ্ছি — আপনি মাত্র **২টা file** copy করবেন, তারপর **১টা command** চালালেই পুরো project (সব folder + file) automatic তৈরি হয়ে যাবে। Download-এর মতোই easy।

## 📥 File 1 — `installer.mjs` (এটা আলাদা file হিসেবে save করুন)

```js
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

const raw = fs.readFileSync('project.txt', 'utf8');
const chunks = raw.split(/^==== FILE:\s*(.+?)\s*====$/gm);
chunks.shift();

const made = [];
for (let i = 0; i < chunks.length; i += 2) {
  const rel = chunks[i].trim();
  let body = chunks[i + 1] ?? '';
  const endIdx = body.indexOf('==== END ====');
  if (endIdx !== -1) body = body.slice(0, endIdx);
  body = body.replace(/^\r?\n/, '').replace(/\r?\n[ \t]*$/, '\n');
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(rel), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(rel, body);
  made.push(rel);
}

if (!fs.existsSync('.env')) fs.copyFileSync('.env.example', '.env');

console.log('✅ ' + made.length + ' files created:');
made.forEach(f => console.log('   ' + f));
console.log('\nএখন করণীয়:');
console.log(' 1) .env ফাইলে আপনার Supabase URL + anon key বসান');
console.log(' 2) npm install');
console.log(' 3) supabase/schema.sql → Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor-এ Run করুন');
console.log(' 4) npm run dev');
```
