## STEP 0 — `supabase/migrations/07_partner_portal.sql`

Screenshot-এর error-এর মূল ফিক্স। Supabase SQL Editor-এ পুরোটা রান করো:

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- 07_PARTNER_PORTAL — full partner system schema
-- ============================================================

-- helper: updated_at
create or replace function public.set_updated_at()
returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin new.updated_at = now(); return new; end $$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1) BUSINESSES (business partner profile — replaces travel_partners misuse)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.businesses (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  owner_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  business_name text not null,
  business_type text not null check (business_type in
    ('hotel','restaurant','guide','agency','transport','operator','shop','activity')),
  category text,
  description_short text,
  description_full text,
  logo_url text,
  cover_url text,
  division text, district text, upazila text, area text,
  address text, postal_code text, map_url text,
  latitude numeric, longitude numeric,
  phone text not null,
  whatsapp text, email text, website text, facebook text, instagram text,
  opening_hours jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,
  status text not null default 'pending'
    check (status in ('pending','approved','rejected','suspended')),
  verification_status text not null default 'unverified'
    check (verification_status in ('unverified','pending','verified','rejected')),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
drop trigger if exists trg_biz_upd on public.businesses;
create trigger trg_biz_upd before update on public.businesses
  for each row execute function public.set_updated_at();

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2) PARTNER SERVICES
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.partner_services (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  business_id uuid not null references public.businesses(id) on delete cascade,
  name text not null,
  category text not null,
  description text not null,
  price numeric(12,2) not null check (price >= 0),
  price_unit text not null default 'fixed'
    check (price_unit in ('night','day','person','trip','fixed')),
  division text, district text, upazila text, area text,
  max_guests int, quantity int,
  cover_url text,
  gallery jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  features jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  status text not null default 'active' check (status in ('active','inactive')),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
drop trigger if exists trg_svc_upd on public.partner_services;
create trigger trg_svc_upd before update on public.partner_services
  for each row execute function public.set_updated_at();

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3) BOOKINGS
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.partner_bookings (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  booking_code text not null unique
    default ('BK-' || upper(substr(md5(random()::text),1,8))),
  business_id uuid not null references public.businesses(id) on delete cascade,
  service_id uuid references public.partner_services(id) on delete set null,
  customer_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  customer_name text not null,
  customer_phone text,
  travel_date date not null,
  guests int not null default 1,
  amount numeric(12,2) not null default 0,
  payment_status text not null default 'unpaid'
    check (payment_status in ('unpaid','partial','paid','refunded')),
  status text not null default 'pending'
    check (status in ('pending','confirmed','completed','cancelled')),
  notes text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
drop trigger if exists trg_bkg_upd on public.partner_bookings;
create trigger trg_bkg_upd before update on public.partner_bookings
  for each row execute function public.set_updated_at();

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4) AVAILABILITY
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.service_availability (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  service_id uuid not null references public.partner_services(id) on delete cascade,
  day date not null,
  state text not null default 'available'
    check (state in ('available','booked','blocked')),
  unique (service_id, day)
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- 5) OFFERS
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.partner_offers (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  business_id uuid not null references public.businesses(id) on delete cascade,
  title text not null,
  discount_type text not null check (discount_type in ('percent','fixed')),
  discount_value numeric(12,2) not null check (discount_value > 0),
  start_date date not null,
  end_date date not null,
  applicable_services jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  min_booking numeric(12,2) not null default 0,
  active boolean not null default true,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- 6) REVIEWS (reply column added — Reply button এখন কাজ করবে)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.partner_reviews (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  business_id uuid not null references public.businesses(id) on delete cascade,
  service_id uuid references public.partner_services(id) on delete set null,
  customer_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  customer_name text not null,
  rating int not null check (rating between 1 and 5),
  comment text,
  reply text,
  replied_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- 7) TRANSACTIONS (finance/payout)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.partner_transactions (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  business_id uuid not null references public.businesses(id) on delete cascade,
  booking_id uuid references public.partner_bookings(id) on delete set null,
  kind text not null check (kind in ('earning','payout','refund')),
  gross numeric(12,2) not null default 0,
  commission numeric(12,2) not null default 0,
  net numeric(12,2) not null default 0,
  status text not null default 'completed'
    check (status in ('pending','processing','completed')),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- 8) VERIFICATION DOCUMENTS
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.partner_documents (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  business_id uuid not null references public.businesses(id) on delete cascade,
  doc_type text not null
    check (doc_type in ('trade_license','nid','registration','property')),
  file_url text not null,
  status text not null default 'pending'
    check (status in ('pending','approved','rejected')),
  submitted_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- 9) COMMUNITY: travel_partner_requests ঠিক রেখে নতুন column +
--    আসল Join request table
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
alter table public.travel_partner_requests
  add column if not exists district text,
  add column if not exists age_group text,
  add column if not exists gender_preference text default 'any',
  add column if not exists destination_text text;

create table if not exists public.travel_partner_joins (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  request_id uuid not null references public.travel_partner_requests(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  message text,
  status text not null default 'requested'
    check (status in ('requested','accepted','declined')),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (request_id, user_id)
);

-- পুরনো ভুল table-টা ডেটা ছাড়া থাকলে বাদ দিতে পারো (comment সরিয়ে):
-- drop table if exists public.travel_partners cascade;

-- ============================================================
-- INDEXES
-- ============================================================
create index if not exists idx_ps_business on public.partner_services(business_id);
create index if not exists idx_pb_business_status on public.partner_bookings(business_id,status);
create index if not exists idx_sa_service_day on public.service_availability(service_id,day);
create index if not exists idx_pr_business on public.partner_reviews(business_id);
create index if not exists idx_ptx_business on public.partner_transactions(business_id);
create index if not exists idx_tpr_status on public.travel_partner_requests(status);

-- ============================================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY
-- ============================================================
alter table public.businesses           enable row level security;
alter table public.partner_services     enable row level security;
alter table public.partner_bookings     enable row level security;
alter table public.service_availability enable row level security;
alter table public.partner_offers       enable row level security;
alter table public.partner_reviews      enable row level security;
alter table public.partner_transactions enable row level security;
alter table public.partner_documents    enable row level security;
alter table public.travel_partner_joins enable row level security;

-- ---- businesses ----
create policy "biz_owner_all" on public.businesses for all
  to authenticated
  using (owner_id = auth.uid())
  with check (owner_id = auth.uid());
create policy "biz_public_read_approved" on public.businesses
  for select using (status = 'approved');

-- ---- partner_services ----
create policy "svc_owner_all" on public.partner_services for all
  to authenticated
  using (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()))
  with check (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()));
create policy "svc_public_read" on public.partner_services
  for select using (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.status = 'approved'));

-- ---- partner_bookings ----
create policy "bkg_owner_all" on public.partner_bookings for all
  to authenticated
  using (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()))
  with check (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()));
create policy "bkg_customer_insert" on public.partner_bookings
  for insert to authenticated with check (customer_id = auth.uid());
create policy "bkg_customer_read" on public.partner_bookings
  for select to authenticated using (customer_id = auth.uid());

-- ---- service_availability ----
create policy "av_owner_all" on public.service_availability for all
  to authenticated
  using (exists (select 1 from public.partner_services s
                 join public.businesses b on b.id = s.business_id
                 where s.id = service_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()))
  with check (exists (select 1 from public.partner_services s
                 join public.businesses b on b.id = s.business_id
                 where s.id = service_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()));

-- ---- partner_offers ----
create policy "off_owner_all" on public.partner_offers for all
  to authenticated
  using (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()))
  with check (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()));
create policy "off_public_read_active" on public.partner_offers
  for select using (active = true);

-- ---- partner_reviews ----
create policy "rev_owner_all" on public.partner_reviews for all
  to authenticated
  using (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()))
  with check (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()));
create policy "rev_customer_insert" on public.partner_reviews
  for insert to authenticated with check (customer_id = auth.uid());
create policy "rev_public_read" on public.partner_reviews
  for select using (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.status = 'approved'));

-- ---- partner_transactions ----
create policy "tx_owner_all" on public.partner_transactions for all
  to authenticated
  using (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()))
  with check (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()));

-- ---- partner_documents ----
create policy "doc_owner_all" on public.partner_documents for all
  to authenticated
  using (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()))
  with check (exists (select 1 from public.businesses b
                 where b.id = business_id and b.owner_id = auth.uid()));

-- ---- travel_partner_joins ----
create policy "join_user_all" on public.travel_partner_joins for all
  to authenticated
  using (user_id = auth.uid())
  with check (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "join_request_owner_read" on public.travel_partner_joins
  for select to authenticated
  using (exists (select 1 from public.travel_partner_requests r
                 where r.id = request_id and r.user_id = auth.uid()));

-- ============================================================
-- STORAGE (একটা public bucket — media + docs)
-- ============================================================
insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public)
values ('partner-media','partner-media', true)
on conflict (id) do nothing;

create policy "media_auth_upload" on storage.objects
  for insert to authenticated with check
  (bucket_id = 'partner-media' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
create policy "media_auth_update" on storage.objects
  for update to authenticated using
  (bucket_id = 'partner-media' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
create policy "media_auth_delete" on storage.objects
  for delete to authenticated using
  (bucket_id = 'partner-media' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
```

## STEP 1 — `src/lib/apiError.js`

Screenshot-এর মতো raw database error আর কখনো user-কে দেখাবে না:

```js
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

/** Raw error console-এ, user-friendly message UI-তে */
export function friendlyError(err) {
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
  console.error('[API ERROR]', err?.code, err?.message, err);
  const code = err?.code || '';
  const msg = String(err?.message || err || '');

  if (msg === 'NO_BUSINESS') return 'আপনার business profile এখনো সম্পূর্ণ হয়নি।';
  if (msg.includes('JWT') || code === 'PGRST301')
    return 'Session শেষ হয়ে গেছে। আবার login করুন।';
  if (code === '23505') return 'এই entry টি আগেই যোগ করা হয়েছে।';
  if (code === '42501' || msg.includes('row-level'))
    return 'আপনার এই কাজটির অনুমতি নেই।';
  if (code === 'PGRST204' || msg.includes('schema cache') ||
      msg.includes('Could not find the') || msg.includes('column'))
    return 'Server-এ সমস্যা হচ্ছে। কিছুক্ষণ পর আবার চেষ্টা করুন।';
  if (msg.includes('Failed to fetch') || msg.includes('NetworkError'))
    return 'ইন্টারনেট সংযোগ পরীক্ষা করুন।';
  return msg.length > 0 && msg.length < 80 ? msg : 'কিছু একটা সমস্যা হয়েছে। আবার চেষ্টা করুন।';
}

export const alertErr = (err) => toast.error(friendlyError(err));
export const alertOk  = (msg) => toast.success(msg);
```

## STEP 3 — `src/components/ui.jsx`

Typography spec অনুযায়ী compact design system (page title 20px, body 14px, input 42px):

```jsx
import { forwardRef, useEffect } from 'react';

export const cx = (...c) => c.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
export const tk = (n) => '৳' + Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('bn-IN');

/* ── Spinner ── */
export const Spinner = ({ className = '' }) => (
  <svg className={cx('h-4 w-4 animate-spin', className)} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none">
    <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeOpacity=".25" strokeWidth="4"/>
    <path d="M22 12a10 10 0 0 1-10 10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" strokeLinecap="round"/>
  </svg>
);

/* ── Button ── */
const BTN_V = {
  primary: 'bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:bg-emerald-300',
  outline: 'border border-slate-300 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50',
  ghost: 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100',
  danger: 'border border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100',
};
const BTN_S = { xs: 'h-7 px-2.5 text-xs', sm: 'h-8 px-3 text-xs', md: 'h-10 px-4 text-sm' };

export const Button = forwardRef(function Button(
  { variant = 'primary', size = 'md', loading, children, className = '', disabled, ...rest }, ref
) {
  return (
    <button ref={ref} disabled={disabled || loading}
      className={cx('inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg font-medium transition disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-70',
        BTN_V[variant], BTN_S[size], className)} {...rest}>
      {loading && <Spinner className="!h-3.5 !w-3.5" />}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
});

/* ── Form primitives (h-42px, text-sm) ── */
const BASE = 'w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 text-sm text-slate-800 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-emerald-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500/25 disabled:bg-slate-50 disabled:text-slate-400';

export const Input    = forwardRef(({ className = '', ...p }, ref) => <input ref={ref} {...p} className={cx(BASE, 'h-[42px]', className)} />);
export const Select   = forwardRef(({ className = '', children, ...p }, ref) => <select ref={ref} {...p} className={cx(BASE, 'h-[42px] pr-8', className)}>{children}</select>);
export const Textarea = forwardRef(({ rows = 3, className = '', ...p }, ref) => <textarea ref={ref} rows={rows} {...p} className={cx(BASE, 'py-2 leading-relaxed', className)} />);

export function Label({ children, required }) {
  return <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">
    {children}{required && <span className="text-red-500"> *</span>}
  </label>;
}
export function Field({ label, required, hint, error, children, className = '' }) {
  return (
    <div className={className}>
      {label && <Label required={required}>{label}</Label>}
      {children}
      {error ? <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-500">{error}</p>
             : hint ? <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">{hint}</p> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Layout ── */
export const Card = ({ className = '', ...p }) => (
  <div {...p} className={cx('rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white', className)} />
);

export function PageHeader({ title, desc, right }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-slate-900">{title}</h1>
        {desc && <p className="mt-0.5 text-sm text-slate-500">{desc}</p>}
      </div>
      {right && <div className="flex items-center gap-2">{right}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Badge ── */
const TONES = {
  green: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 ring-emerald-200',
  amber: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 ring-amber-200',
  red: 'bg-red-50 text-red-600 ring-red-200',
  blue: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 ring-blue-200',
  slate: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 ring-slate-200',
};
export const Badge = ({ tone = 'slate', children }) => (
  <span className={cx('inline-flex items-center rounded-md px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ring-1', TONES[tone])}>
    {children}
  </span>
);

/* ── StatCard ── */
export function StatCard({ icon: Icon, label, value, sub }) {
  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">{label}</p>
          <p className="mt-1 truncate text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">{value}</p>
          {sub && <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-400">{sub}</p>}
        </div>
        {Icon && <div className="rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 p-2 text-emerald-600"><Icon size={18} /></div>}
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

/* ── EmptyState ── */
export function EmptyState({ icon: Icon, title, desc, action }) {
  return (
    <Card className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 text-center">
      <div className="mb-3 rounded-full bg-slate-100 p-3 text-slate-400">{Icon && <Icon size={22} />}</div>
      <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">{title}</h3>
      {desc && <p className="mt-1 max-w-sm text-sm text-slate-500">{desc}</p>}
      {action && <div className="mt-4">{action}</div>}
    </Card>
  );
}

/* ── Modal ── */
export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, children, width = 'max-w-lg' }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const h = (e) => e.key === 'Escape' && onClose?.();
    if (open) window.addEventListener('keydown', h);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', h);
  }, [open, onClose]);
  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className={cx('relative w-full rounded-xl bg-white shadow-xl', width)}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-3">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{title}</h3>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="rounded p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-600">✕</button>
        </div>
        <div className="max-h-[75vh] overflow-y-auto p-5">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Tabs ── */
export function Tabs({ tabs, value, onChange }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex w-fit flex-wrap gap-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-1">
      {tabs.map((t) => (
        <button key={t.value} onClick={() => onChange(t.value)}
          className={cx('rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition',
            value === t.value ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100')}>
          {t.label}{t.count != null && <span className="ml-1 opacity-70">({t.count})</span>}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Section ── */
export function Section({ title, children, right }) {
  return (
    <Card className="p-5">
      {(title || right) &&
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-800">{title}</h2>{right}
        </div>}
      {children}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

## STEP 4 — `src/components/SearchableSelect.jsx`

৬৪ জেলার giant native select-এর বদলে searchable dropdown:

```jsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { cx } from './ui';

export default function SearchableSelect({
  options = [], value, onChange, placeholder = 'Select…', searchPlaceholder = '🔍 Search…', disabled,
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const ref = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = (e) => { if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target)) setOpen(false); };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', h);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', h);
  }, []);

  const filtered = options.filter((o) => o.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()));

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={ref}>
      <button type="button" disabled={disabled}
        onClick={() => { setOpen((o) => !o); setQ(''); }}
        className={cx(
          'flex h-[42px] w-full items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 text-left text-sm transition',
          disabled ? 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-400' : 'hover:border-slate-400',
          value ? 'text-slate-800' : 'text-slate-400'
        )}>
        <span className="truncate">{value || placeholder}</span>
        <span className="ml-2 text-slate-400">▾</span>
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div className="absolute z-40 mt-1 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-lg">
          <div className="border-b border-slate-100 p-2">
            <input autoFocus value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={searchPlaceholder}
              className="h-9 w-full rounded-md border border-slate-200 px-3 text-sm focus:border-emerald-500 focus:outline-none" />
          </div>
          <div className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto py-1">
            {filtered.length === 0 && (
              <p className="px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-400">কিছু পাওয়া যায়নি</p>
            )}
            {filtered.map((o) => (
              <button type="button" key={o}
                onClick={() => { onChange(o); setOpen(false); }}
                className={cx('block w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-emerald-50',
                  o === value && 'bg-emerald-50 font-medium text-emerald-700')}>
                {o}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

## STEP 5 — `src/components/LocationPicker.jsx`

```jsx
import { Field } from './ui';
import SearchableSelect from './SearchableSelect';
import { DIVISIONS, DISTRICTS_OF, UPAZILAS_OF } from '../data/locationData';

export default function LocationPicker({ value = {}, onChange }) {
  const set = (patch) => onChange({ ...value, ...patch });
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
      <Field label="Division" required>
        <SearchableSelect placeholder="বিভাগ নির্বাচন করুন"
          options={DIVISIONS} value={value.division || ''}
          onChange={(v) => set({ division: v, district: '', upazila: '' })} />
      </Field>
      <Field label="District" required>
        <SearchableSelect placeholder="জেলা নির্বাচন করুন"
          disabled={!value.division}
          options={DISTRICTS_OF(value.division)} value={value.district || ''}
          onChange={(v) => set({ district: v, upazila: '' })} />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Upazila">
        <SearchableSelect placeholder="উপজেলা নির্বাচন করুন"
          disabled={!value.district}
          options={UPAZILAS_OF(value.district)} value={value.upazila || ''}
          onChange={(v) => set({ upazila: v })} />
      </Field>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## STEP 6 — `src/components/ImageUploader.jsx`

```jsx
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';   // ← তোমার client-এর path অনুযায়ী adjust করো
import { Button } from './ui';
import { alertErr } from '../lib/apiError';

export default function ImageUploader({ value, onUploaded, mode = 'image', label = 'Upload', className = '' }) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef(null);

  async function handle(e) {
    const f = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!f) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      const path = `${user.id}/${Date.now()}-${f.name.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}`;
      const { error } = await supabase.storage.from('partner-media').upload(path, f);
      if (error) throw error;
      const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage.from('partner-media').getPublicUrl(path);
      onUploaded(publicUrl);
    } catch (err) {
      alertErr(err);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
      if (ref.current) ref.current.value = '';
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className={className}>
      <input ref={ref} type="file" hidden
        accept={mode === 'image' ? 'image/*' : '.pdf,.jpg,.jpeg,.png'} onChange={handle} />
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {value && (mode === 'image'
          ? <img src={value} alt="" className="h-14 w-14 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 object-cover" />
          : <a href={value} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"
              className="text-xs font-medium text-emerald-600 underline">View file ↗</a>)}
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" type="button" loading={busy}
          onClick={() => ref.current?.click()}>
          {value ? 'Change' : label}
        </Button>
        {value && mode === 'image' && (
          <button type="button" onClick={() => onUploaded('')}
            className="text-xs text-red-500 hover:underline">Remove</button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## STEP 7 — `src/services/partnerService.js`

সব query owner-scoped — আর কোথাও `travel_partners` নেই:

```js
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';

const COMMISSION_RATE = 0.10;

/* ─────────── Business ─────────── */
export async function getCurrentUser() {
  const { data } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  return data.user ?? null;
}

export async function getMyBusiness() {
  const u = await getCurrentUser();
  if (!u) return null;
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('businesses').select('*').eq('owner_id', u.id).maybeSingle();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

async function biz() {
  const b = await getMyBusiness();
  if (!b) { const e = new Error('NO_BUSINESS'); e.code = 'NO_BUSINESS'; throw e; }
  return b;
}

export async function saveBusiness(values) {
  const b = await biz();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('businesses').update(values).eq('id', b.id).select().single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

/* ─────────── Services ─────────── */
export async function listServices() {
  const b = await biz();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_services').select('*')
    .eq('business_id', b.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function getService(id) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_services').select('*').eq('id', id).maybeSingle();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function saveService(values, id) {
  const b = await biz();
  if (id) {
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('partner_services').update(values).eq('id', id);
    if (error) throw error;
    return id;
  }
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_services').insert({ ...values, business_id: b.id }).select().single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data.id;
}

export async function setServiceStatus(id, status) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('partner_services').update({ status }).eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw error;
}

export async function deleteService(id) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('partner_services').delete().eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw error;
}

/* ─────────── Bookings ─────────── */
export async function listBookings() {
  const b = await biz();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_bookings').select('*, service:partner_services(name)')
    .eq('business_id', b.id)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(200);
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function recentBookings(limit = 5) {
  const b = await biz();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_bookings').select('*, service:partner_services(name)')
    .eq('business_id', b.id)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(limit);
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function updateBookingStatus(id, status) {
  const b = await biz();
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_bookings').update({ status }).eq('id', id).eq('business_id', b.id);
  if (error) throw error;

  // Booking complete হলে earning transaction তৈরি (duplicate guard সহ)
  if (status === 'completed') {
    const { data: existing } = await supabase
      .from('partner_transactions').select('id').eq('booking_id', id).eq('kind', 'earning');
    if (!existing?.length) {
      const { data: bk } = await supabase
        .from('partner_bookings').select('amount').eq('id', id).single();
      const gross = Number(bk?.amount || 0);
      const commission = +(gross * COMMISSION_RATE).toFixed(2);
      await supabase.from('partner_transactions').insert({
        business_id: b.id, booking_id: id, kind: 'earning',
        gross, commission, net: +(gross - commission).toFixed(2), status: 'completed',
      });
    }
  }
}

/* ─────────── Availability ─────────── */
export async function listAvailability(serviceId, from, to) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('service_availability').select('day,state')
    .eq('service_id', serviceId).gte('day', from).lte('day', to);
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function setDayState(serviceId, day, state) {
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('service_availability').upsert(
      { service_id: serviceId, day, state },
      { onConflict: 'service_id,day' }
    );
  if (error) throw error;
}

/* ─────────── Offers ─────────── */
export async function listOffers() {
  const b = await biz();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_offers').select('*')
    .eq('business_id', b.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function createOffer(v) {
  const b = await biz();
  const { error } = await supabase.from('partner_offers').insert({ ...v, business_id: b.id });
  if (error) throw error;
}
export async function toggleOffer(id, active) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('partner_offers').update({ active }).eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw error;
}
export async function deleteOffer(id) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('partner_offers').delete().eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw error;
}

/* ─────────── Reviews ─────────── */
export async function listReviews() {
  const b = await biz();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_reviews').select('*, service:partner_services(name)')
    .eq('business_id', b.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function replyReview(id, reply) {
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_reviews').update({ reply, replied_at: new Date().toISOString() })
    .eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw error;
}

/* ─────────── Finance ─────────── */
export async function listTransactions() {
  const b = await biz();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_transactions').select('*, booking:partner_bookings(booking_code)')
    .eq('business_id', b.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(200);
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function requestPayout(amount) {
  const b = await biz();
  const { error } = await supabase.from('partner_transactions').insert({
    business_id: b.id, kind: 'payout', net: Number(amount),
    gross: Number(amount), commission: 0, status: 'pending',
  });
  if (error) throw error;
}

/* ─────────── Verification ─────────── */
export async function listDocuments() {
  const b = await biz();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_documents').select('*')
    .eq('business_id', b.id).order('submitted_at', { ascending: false });
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function addDocument(doc_type, file_url) {
  const b = await biz();
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_documents').insert({ business_id: b.id, doc_type, file_url });
  if (error) throw error;
}

export async function submitForVerification() {
  const b = await biz();
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('businesses').update({ verification_status: 'pending' }).eq('id', b.id);
  if (error) throw error;
}

/* ─────────── Stats (Dashboard + Analytics) ─────────── */
const countOf = async (table, match) => {
  const { count } = await supabase.from(table).select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).match(match);
  return count || 0;
};

export async function getStats() {
  const b = await biz();
  const [services, pending, confirmed, completed] = await Promise.all([
    countOf('partner_services', { business_id: b.id }),
    countOf('partner_bookings', { business_id: b.id, status: 'pending' }),
    countOf('partner_bookings', { business_id: b.id, status: 'confirmed' }),
    countOf('partner_bookings', { business_id: b.id, status: 'completed' }),
  ]);
  const [{ data: txs }, { data: revs }] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from('partner_transactions').select('kind,status,net')
      .eq('business_id', b.id).eq('kind', 'earning').eq('status', 'completed'),
    supabase.from('partner_reviews').select('rating').eq('business_id', b.id),
  ]);
  const revenue = (txs || []).reduce((s, t) => s + Number(t.net || 0), 0);
  const ratings = (revs || []).map((r) => r.rating);
  const avgRating = ratings.length
    ? +(ratings.reduce((s, r) => s + r, 0) / ratings.length).toFixed(1) : null;
  return { services, pending, confirmed, completed, revenue, reviewCount: ratings.length, avgRating };
}

/** Analytics — last N days bookings + revenue */
export async function getChartData(days = 30) {
  const b = await biz();
  const since = new Date(Date.now() - days * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('partner_bookings').select('travel_date,status,amount,guests')
    .eq('business_id', b.id).gte('travel_date', since);
  if (error) throw error;
  const buckets = {};
  for (let i = days - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const d = new Date(Date.now() - i * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    buckets[d] = { bookings: 0, revenue: 0 };
  }
  (data || []).forEach((r) => {
    if (buckets[r.travel_date]) {
      buckets[r.travel_date].bookings += 1;
      buckets[r.travel_date].revenue += Number(r.amount || 0);
    }
  });
  return buckets;
}
```

## STEP 8 — `src/services/communityService.js` (FIXED)

Table name mismatch + status mismatch + missing gender filter — সব ফিক্স:

```js
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';

// ✅ migration 06-এর সাথে মিলে যাওয়া table
const TABLE = 'travel_partner_requests';

/** Traveler-to-traveler request list — আসল filter সহ */
export async function listRequests(filters = {}) {
  let q = supabase
    .from(TABLE).select('*')
    .eq('status', 'active')                       // ✅ migration default = 'active'
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(60);
  if (filters.district) q = q.eq('district', filters.district);
  if (filters.gender)   q = q.eq('gender_preference', filters.gender);
  if (filters.ageGroup) q = q.eq('age_group', filters.ageGroup);
  const { data, error } = await q;
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function createRequest(payload) {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from(TABLE).insert({ ...payload, user_id: user.id, status: 'active' })
    .select().single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function myRequests() {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from(TABLE).select('*').eq('user_id', user.id)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}

export async function closeRequest(id) {
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from(TABLE).update({ status: 'closed' }).eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw error;
}

/** ✅ আসল Join — travel_partner_joins-এ insert হয়, শুধু toast না */
export async function joinRequest(requestId, message = '') {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('travel_partner_joins')
    .insert({ request_id: requestId, user_id: user.id, message, status: 'requested' });
  if (error) {
    if (error.code === '23505') {
      const dup = new Error('ALREADY_JOINED'); dup.code = 'ALREADY_JOINED'; throw dup;
    }
    throw error;
  }
  return true;
}

/** কোন কোন request-এ ইতিমধ্যে join করেছি */
export async function myJoinedIds(ids) {
  if (!ids?.length) return new Set();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('travel_partner_joins').select('request_id')
    .eq('user_id', user.id).in('request_id', ids);
  if (error) throw error;
  return new Set((data || []).map((d) => d.request_id));
}
```

## STEP 9 — `src/pages/partner/PartnerPortalLayout.jsx`

Sidebar-এ **শুধু real pages** (Marketing/Affiliate/Team বাদ), logout handler সহ:

```jsx
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  LayoutGrid, Building2, Package, ClipboardList, CalendarRange, TicketPercent,
  Star, MessageSquare, Wallet, BarChart3, ShieldCheck, Settings, LogOut, Menu, X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { getMyBusiness } from '../../services/partnerService';
import { alertErr } from '../../lib/apiError';
import { cx, Spinner } from '../../components/ui';

const Ctx = createContext(null);
export const usePartner = () => useContext(Ctx);

const NAV = [
  ['/partner/dashboard',    LayoutGrid,    'Dashboard'],
  ['/partner/profile',      Building2,     'Business Profile'],
  ['/partner/services',     Package,       'Services'],
  ['/partner/bookings',     ClipboardList, 'Bookings'],
  ['/partner/availability', CalendarRange, 'Availability'],
  ['/partner/offers',       TicketPercent, 'Offers'],
  ['/partner/reviews',      Star,          'Reviews'],
  ['/partner/messages',     MessageSquare, 'Messages'],
  ['/partner/finance',      Wallet,        'Finance'],
  ['/partner/analytics',    BarChart3,     'Analytics'],
  ['/partner/verification', ShieldCheck,   'Verification'],
  ['/partner/settings',     Settings,      'Settings'],
];

function NavItems({ onNavigate }) {
  return (
    <nav className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5 overflow-y-auto p-3">
      {NAV.map(([to, Icon, label]) => (
        <NavLink key={to} to={to} onClick={onNavigate}
          className={({ isActive }) => cx(
            'flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition',
            isActive ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
                     : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900'
          )}>
          <Icon size={17} className="shrink-0" />
          {label}
        </NavLink>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}

export default function PartnerPortalLayout() {
  const [business, setBusiness] = useState(undefined); // undefined = loading
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    try { setBusiness(await getMyBusiness()); }
    catch (err) { alertErr(err); setBusiness(null); }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { reload(); }, [reload]);

  async function handleLogout() {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    toast.success('Logged out');
    navigate('/');
  }

  if (business === undefined) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-slate-50 text-emerald-600">
        <Spinner className="h-6 w-6" />
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (!business) return <Navigate to="/partner/register" replace />;

  return (
    <Ctx.Provider value={{ business, setBusiness, reload }}>
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50">
        {/* Mobile topbar */}
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-3 lg:hidden">
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">Partner Portal</span>
          <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)} className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100">
            <Menu size={20} />
          </button>
        </header>

        {/* Mobile drawer */}
        {mobileOpen && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-40 lg:hidden">
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/40" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />
            <aside className="relative flex h-full w-64 flex-col bg-white">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3">
                <span className="font-semibold">Partner Portal</span>
                <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} className="p-1 text-slate-400"><X size={18} /></button>
              </div>
              <NavItems onNavigate={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />
              <div className="border-t border-slate-200 p-3">
                <button onClick={handleLogout}
                  className="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50">
                  <LogOut size={17} /> Logout
                </button>
              </div>
            </aside>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Desktop sidebar */}
        <aside className="fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-30 hidden w-60 flex-col border-r border-slate-200 bg-white lg:flex">
          <div className="flex h-14 items-center gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 px-4">
            <div className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-sm font-bold text-white">M</div>
            <div className="leading-tight">
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Madventure</p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Partner Portal</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <NavItems />
          <div className="border-t border-slate-200 p-3">
            <button onClick={handleLogout}
              className="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50">
              <LogOut size={17} /> Logout
            </button>
          </div>
        </aside>

        {/* Content */}
        <main className="lg:pl-60">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl p-4 sm:p-6">
            <Outlet />
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </Ctx.Provider>
  );
}
```

## STEP 10 — `src/pages/partner/PartnerDashboard.jsx` (Overview)

```jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { CalendarCheck, Package, Star, Wallet, Plus, Building2, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { usePartner } from './PartnerPortalLayout';
import { getStats, recentBookings } from '../../services/partnerService';
import { PageHeader, StatCard, Card, Badge, EmptyState, tk } from '../../components/ui';
import { alertErr } from '../../lib/apiError';

const STATUS_TONE = { pending: 'amber', confirmed: 'blue', completed: 'green', cancelled: 'red' };

export default function PartnerDashboard() {
  const { business } = usePartner();
  const [stats, setStats] = useState(null);
  const [recent, setRecent] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    getStats().then(setStats).catch(alertErr);
    recentBookings(5).then(setRecent).catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title={`স্বাগতম, ${business.business_name}`}
        desc={`${business.district || ''}${business.district ? ', ' : ''}${business.division || ''}`}
        right={
          <>
            <Badge tone={business.status === 'approved' ? 'green' : 'amber'}>
              {business.status === 'approved' ? 'Approved' : business.status === 'pending' ? 'Pending review' : business.status}
            </Badge>
            <Link to="/partner/services">
              <span className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 px-3.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-emerald-700">
                <Plus size={15} /> Add Service
              </span>
            </Link>
          </>
        }
      />

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
        <StatCard icon={CalendarCheck} label="Pending bookings" value={stats ? stats.pending : '—'} sub="Action needed" />
        <StatCard icon={Package} label="Active services" value={stats ? stats.services : '—'} />
        <StatCard icon={Wallet} label="Net earnings" value={stats ? tk(stats.revenue) : '—'} />
        <StatCard icon={Star} label="Avg rating" value={stats?.avgRating ?? '—'} sub={stats ? `${stats.reviewCount} reviews` : ''} />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <Card className="p-5 lg:col-span-2">
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-800">Recent bookings</h2>
            <Link to="/partner/bookings" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-600 hover:underline">
              View all <ArrowRight size={13} />
            </Link>
          </div>
          {recent.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState icon={CalendarCheck} title="এখনো কোনো booking নেই"
              desc="Service যোগ করার পর customer booking করলে এখানে দেখা যাবে।"
              action={<Link to="/partner/services" className="text-sm font-medium text-emerald-600 hover:underline">Add your first service →</Link>} />
          ) : (
            <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {recent.map((bk) => (
                <div key={bk.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-2.5">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="truncate text-sm font-medium text-slate-800">
                      {bk.customer_name} · {bk.service?.name || 'Service'}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                      {bk.booking_code} · {bk.travel_date} · {bk.guests} guests
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{tk(bk.amount)}</span>
                    <Badge tone={STATUS_TONE[bk.status]}>{bk.status}</Badge>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </Card>

        <Card className="p-5">
          <h2 className="mb-3 text-base font-semibold text-slate-800">Quick actions</h2>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {[
              ['/partner/profile', Building2, 'Complete business profile'],
              ['/partner/services', Package, 'Manage services'],
              ['/partner/offers', TicketPercent, 'Create an offer'],
              ['/partner/finance', Wallet, 'Request payout'],
            ].map(([to, Icon, label]) => (
              <Link key={to} to={to}
                className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-50">
                <Icon size={16} className="text-emerald-600" /> {label}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
```

## STEP 11 — `src/pages/partner/BusinessProfile.jsx`

Full save functionality + cascading location + opening hours:

```jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { usePartner } from './PartnerPortalLayout';
import { saveBusiness } from '../../services/partnerService';
import { alertErr, alertOk } from '../../lib/apiError';
import {
  PageHeader, Section, Field, Input, Textarea, Button, ImageUploader,
} from '../../components/ui';
import LocationPicker from '../../components/LocationPicker';

export const BUSINESS_TYPES = [
  ['hotel', 'Hotel / Resort'], ['restaurant', 'Restaurant'], ['guide', 'Tour Guide'],
  ['agency', 'Travel Agency'], ['transport', 'Transport / Rent-a-Car'],
  ['operator', 'Tour Operator'], ['shop', 'Shop'], ['activity', 'Activity / Adventure'],
];

const DAYS = ['sat', 'sun', 'mon', 'tue', 'wed', 'thu', 'fri'];

export default function BusinessProfile() {
  const { business, setBusiness } = usePartner();
  const [form, setForm] = useState(() => ({
    ...business,
    opening_hours: business.opening_hours || {},
  }));
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const set = (k) => (e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: e?.target ? e.target.value : e }));

  async function handleSave(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const updated = await saveBusiness(form);
      setBusiness(updated);
      alertOk('Business profile saved ✓');
    } catch (err) { alertErr(err); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  }

  const hours = form.opening_hours || {};
  const setHours = (day, patch) =>
    setForm((f) => ({
      ...f,
      opening_hours: { ...f.opening_hours, [day]: { ...(f.opening_hours?.[day] || {}), ...patch } },
    }));

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSave}>
      <PageHeader title="Business Profile" desc="তোমার business-এর সব তথ্য এখানে manage করো"
        right={<Button type="submit" loading={saving}>Save Changes</Button>} />

      <div className="space-y-5">
        <Section title="Basic Information">
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="Business Name" required>
              <Input value={form.business_name || ''} onChange={set('business_name')} required />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Business Type" required>
              <select value={form.business_type || ''} onChange={set('business_type')} required
                className="h-[42px] w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 text-sm focus:border-emerald-500 focus:outline-none">
                {BUSINESS_TYPES.map(([v, l]) => <option key={v} value={v}>{l}</option>)}
              </select>
            </Field>
            <Field label="Category" className="sm:col-span-2"
              hint="যেমন: Sea-view hotel, Street food, Hill trekking">
              <Input value={form.category || ''} onChange={set('category')} />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Short Description" className="sm:col-span-2" hint="সর্বোচ্চ ১৬০ অক্ষর — listing card-এ দেখাবে">
              <Input maxLength={160} value={form.description_short || ''} onChange={set('description_short')} />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Full Description" className="sm:col-span-2">
              <Textarea rows={4} value={form.description_full || ''} onChange={set('description_full')} />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Logo">
              <ImageUploader value={form.logo_url || ''} onUploaded={set('logo_url')} label="Upload logo" />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Cover Image">
              <ImageUploader value={form.cover_url || ''} onUploaded={set('cover_url')} label="Upload cover" />
            </Field>
          </div>
        </Section>

        <Section title="Location">
          <LocationPicker value={form} onChange={(v) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, ...v }))} />
          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="Full Address" className="sm:col-span-2">
              <Input value={form.address || ''} onChange={set('address')} />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Postal Code"><Input value={form.postal_code || ''} onChange={set('postal_code')} /></Field>
            <Field label="Google Maps Link"><Input value={form.map_url || ''} onChange={set('map_url')} placeholder="https://maps.google.com/…" /></Field>
            <Field label="Latitude"><Input type="number" step="any" value={form.latitude ?? ''} onChange={set('latitude')} /></Field>
            <Field label="Longitude"><Input type="number" step="any" value={form.longitude ?? ''} onChange={set('longitude')} /></Field>
          </div>
        </Section>

        <Section title="Contact Information">
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="Phone" required><Input value={form.phone || ''} onChange={set('phone')} required /></Field>
            <Field label="WhatsApp" hint="থাকলে customer-দের WhatsApp button দেখাবে">
              <Input value={form.whatsapp || ''} onChange={set('whatsapp')} />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Email"><Input type="email" value={form.email || ''} onChange={set('email')} /></Field>
            <Field label="Website"><Input value={form.website || ''} onChange={set('website')} placeholder="https://…" /></Field>
            <Field label="Facebook"><Input value={form.facebook || ''} onChange={set('facebook')} placeholder="https://facebook.com/…" /></Field>
            <Field label="Instagram"><Input value={form.instagram || ''} onChange={set('instagram')} placeholder="https://instagram.com/…" /></Field>
          </div>
        </Section>

        <Section title="Opening Hours">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {DAYS.map((d) => (
              <div key={d} className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="w-10 text-xs font-medium capitalize text-slate-600">{d}</span>
                <input type="checkbox" checked={!!hours[d]?.closed}
                  onChange={(e) => setHours(d, { closed: e.target.checked })}
                  className="h-4 w-4 accent-emerald-600" />
                <span className="w-12 text-xs text-slate-500">Closed</span>
                <input type="time" disabled={hours[d]?.closed} value={hours[d]?.open || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setHours(d, { open: e.target.value })}
                  className="h-9 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-2 text-sm disabled:bg-slate-50" />
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">–</span>
                <input type="time" disabled={hours[d]?.closed} value={hours[d]?.close || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setHours(d, { close: e.target.value })}
                  className="h-9 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-2 text-sm disabled:bg-slate-50" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Section>

        <div className="flex justify-end">
          <Button type="submit" loading={saving}>Save Changes</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

## STEP 12 — `src/pages/partner/Services.jsx`

```jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Plus, Package, Pencil, Trash2, CalendarRange, Power } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { listServices, setServiceStatus, deleteService } from '../../services/partnerService';
import { PageHeader, Card, Badge, Button, EmptyState, tk, Modal } from '../../components/ui';
import { alertErr, alertOk } from '../../lib/apiError';

const CAT_LABEL = {
  stay: 'Stay', food: 'Food', tour: 'Tour / Guide',
  transport: 'Transport', activity: 'Activity', product: 'Product', other: 'Other',
};
const UNIT_LABEL = { night: '/night', day: '/day', person: '/person', trip: '/trip', fixed: '' };

export default function Services() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState(null);
  const [delTarget, setDelTarget] = useState(null);
  const [busyDel, setBusyDel] = useState(false);

  const load = () => listServices().then(setItems).catch(alertErr);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  async function toggle(svc) {
    try {
      await setServiceStatus(svc.id, svc.status === 'active' ? 'inactive' : 'active');
      load();
    } catch (err) { alertErr(err); }
  }

  async function confirmDelete() {
    setBusyDel(true);
    try {
      await deleteService(delTarget.id);
      toast.success('Service deleted');
      setDelTarget(null);
      load();
    } catch (err) { alertErr(err); }
    finally { setBusyDel(false); }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="My Services" desc="তোমার সব service/product এখানে"
        right={
          <Link to="/partner/services/new">
            <span className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 px-3.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-emerald-700">
              <Plus size={15} /> Add Service
            </span>
          </Link>
        } />

      {items === null ? (
        <Card className="p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-400">Loading…</Card>
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={Package} title="কোনো service নেই"
          desc="প্রথম service যোগ করো — room, tour, transport যা-ই হোক।"
          action={<Link to="/partner/services/new" className="text-sm font-medium text-emerald-600 hover:underline">+ Add Service</Link>} />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          {items.map((svc) => (
            <Card key={svc.id} className="overflow-hidden">
              <div className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center bg-slate-100">
                {svc.cover_url
                  ? <img src={svc.cover_url} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                  : <Package size={26} className="text-slate-300" />}
              </div>
              <div className="p-4">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <h3 className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">{svc.name}</h3>
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-400">{CAT_LABEL[svc.category] || svc.category}</p>
                  </div>
                  <Badge tone={svc.status === 'active' ? 'green' : 'slate'}>{svc.status}</Badge>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-2 text-base font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                  {tk(svc.price)} <span className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-400">{UNIT_LABEL[svc.price_unit]}</span>
                </p>
                <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  <Link to={`/partner/services/${svc.id}/edit`}>
                    <Button variant="outline" size="xs"><Pencil size={13} /> Edit</Button>
                  </Link>
                  <Link to={`/partner/availability?service=${svc.id}`}>
                    <Button variant="outline" size="xs"><CalendarRange size={13} /> Availability</Button>
                  </Link>
                  <Button variant="ghost" size="xs" onClick={() => toggle(svc)}>
                    <Power size={13} /> {svc.status === 'active' ? 'Pause' : 'Activate'}
                  </Button>
                  <Button variant="danger" size="xs" onClick={() => setDelTarget(svc)}>
                    <Trash2 size={13} /> Delete
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal open={!!delTarget} onClose={() => setDelTarget(null)} title="Delete service?" width="max-w-sm">
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          <strong>{delTarget?.name}</strong> মুছে ফেলা হবে। এটি আর ফেরানো যাবে না।
        </p>
        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setDelTarget(null)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button variant="danger" loading={busyDel} onClick={confirmDelete}>Delete</Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </>
  );
}
```

## STEP 13 — `src/pages/partner/ServiceForm.jsx`

Category অনুযায়ী **dynamic feature fields** — review-এর point 12 অনুযায়ী:

```jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { getService, saveService } from '../../services/partnerService';
import { alertErr, alertOk } from '../../lib/apiError';
import {
  PageHeader, Section, Field, Input, Textarea, Button, ImageUploader, cx,
} from '../../components/ui';
import LocationPicker from '../../components/LocationPicker';

const CATEGORIES = [
  ['stay', 'Hotel / Stay'], ['food', 'Restaurant / Food'], ['tour', 'Tour / Guide'],
  ['transport', 'Transport'], ['activity', 'Activity / Adventure'],
  ['product', 'Shop / Product'], ['other', 'Other'],
];
const UNITS = [['night', 'Per Night'], ['day', 'Per Day'], ['person', 'Per Person'], ['trip', 'Per Trip'], ['fixed', 'Fixed']];
const FEATURES = {
  stay: ['Wi-Fi', 'AC', 'Breakfast', 'Parking', 'Pool', 'TV', 'Room Service'],
  tour: ['Experienced Guide', 'Transport', 'Food', 'Photography', 'Entry Tickets'],
  transport: ['AC', 'Driver', '4 Seats', '7 Seats', 'Airport Pickup', 'Round Trip'],
  food: ['Home Delivery', 'Outdoor Seating', 'Halal', 'Buffet', 'Wi-Fi'],
  activity: ['Equipment Included', 'Instructor', 'Insurance', 'Photos'],
  product: ['Brand New', 'Delivery Available', 'Warranty'],
  other: ['Customizable'],
};
const EMPTY = {
  name: '', category: 'stay', description: '', price: '', price_unit: 'night',
  max_guests: '', quantity: '', cover_url: '', gallery: [], features: [],
  division: '', district: '', upazila: '', area: '',
};

export default function ServiceForm() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!!id);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const set = (k) => (e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: e?.target ? e.target.value : e }));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!id) return;
    getService(id)
      .then((s) => { if (s) setForm({ ...EMPTY, ...s }); })
      .catch(alertErr)
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [id]);

  const featList = FEATURES[form.category] || FEATURES.other;

  function toggleFeature(feat) {
    setForm((f) => ({
      ...f,
      features: f.features.includes(feat)
        ? f.features.filter((x) => x !== feat)
        : [...f.features, feat],
    }));
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!form.name.trim() || !String(form.price).trim()) {
      alertOk('⚠ Service name ও price দিতে হবে');
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload = {
        ...form,
        price: Number(form.price),
        max_guests: form.max_guests ? Number(form.max_guests) : null,
        quantity: form.quantity ? Number(form.quantity) : null,
      };
      await saveService(payload, id || undefined);
      alertOk(id ? 'Service updated ✓' : 'Service added ✓');
      navigate('/partner/services');
    } catch (err) { alertErr(err); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="py-20 text-center text-sm text-slate-400">Loading…</div>;

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mx-auto max-w-[760px]">
      <PageHeader title={id ? 'Edit Service' : 'Add Service'}
        right={
          <>
            <Link to="/partner/services"><Button variant="outline" type="button">Cancel</Button></Link>
            <Button type="submit" loading={saving}>{id ? 'Update' : 'Create'} Service</Button>
          </>
        } />

      <div className="space-y-5">
        <Section title="Basic">
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="Service Name" required className="sm:col-span-2">
              <Input value={form.name} onChange={set('name')} placeholder="যেমন: Deluxe Sea View Room" required />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Service Category" required>
              <select value={form.category} onChange={set('category')}
                className="h-[42px] w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 text-sm focus:border-emerald-500 focus:outline-none">
                {CATEGORIES.map(([v, l]) => <option key={v} value={v}>{l}</option>)}
              </select>
            </Field>
            <Field label="Description" required className="sm:col-span-2 order-last">
              <Textarea value={form.description} onChange={set('description')}
                placeholder="Service-এর বিবরণ লিখো…" required />
            </Field>
          </div>
        </Section>

        <Section title="Pricing">
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="Price (৳)" required>
              <Input type="number" min="0" value={form.price} onChange={set('price')} required />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Price Unit" required>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {UNITS.map(([v, l]) => (
                  <button key={v} type="button"
                    onClick={() => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, price_unit: v }))}
                    className={cx('rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition',
                      form.price_unit === v
                        ? 'border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                        : 'border-slate-300 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')}>
                    {l}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </Field>
          </div>
        </Section>

        <Section title="Capacity">
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="Maximum Guests" hint="Stay/tour হলে সর্বোচ্চ কত জন">
              <Input type="number" min="1" value={form.max_guests} onChange={set('max_guests')} />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Available Quantity" hint="যেমন: এই room type-এর ৫টা unit আছে">
              <Input type="number" min="1" value={form.quantity} onChange={set('quantity')} />
            </Field>
          </div>
        </Section>

        <Section title="Features" desc={`${form.category} category-এর জন্য applicable options`}>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {featList.map((feat) => (
              <button key={feat} type="button" onClick={() => toggleFeature(feat)}
                className={cx('rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition',
                  form.features.includes(feat)
                    ? 'border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
                    : 'border-slate-300 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')}>
                {form.features.includes(feat) ? '✓ ' : '+ '}{feat}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Section>

        <Section title="Location" desc="Service যদি নির্দিষ্ট জায়গায় হয়">
          <LocationPicker value={form} onChange={(v) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, ...v }))} />
          <Field label="Area / Union" className="mt-4 max-w-xs">
            <Input value={form.area || ''} onChange={set('area')} />
          </Field>
        </Section>

        <Section title="Media">
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <Field label="Cover Image">
              <ImageUploader value={form.cover_url || ''} onUploaded={set('cover_url')} label="Upload cover" />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Gallery Images">
              <ImageUploader label="Add photo"
                onUploaded={(url) => url && setForm((f) => ({ ...f, gallery: [...(f.gallery || []), url] }))} />
              {!!form.gallery?.length && (
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {form.gallery.map((g, i) => (
                    <div key={i} className="relative">
                      <img src={g} alt="" className="h-16 w-16 rounded-lg border object-cover" />
                      <button type="button"
                        onClick={() => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, gallery: f.gallery.filter((_, j) => j !== i) }))}
                        className="absolute -right-1.5 -top-1.5 grid h-5 w-5 place-items-center rounded-full bg-red-500 text-[10px] text-white">✕</button>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </Field>
          </div>
        </Section>

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pb-4">
          <Link to="/partner/services"><Button variant="outline" type="button">Cancel</Button></Link>
          <Button type="submit" loading={saving}>{id ? 'Update Service' : 'Create Service'}</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

## STEP 14 — `src/pages/partner/Bookings.jsx`

সব action button **আসলে কাজ করে** — আর কোনো dead button নেই:

```jsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Eye, Check, X, CheckCircle2, MessageCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { listBookings, updateBookingStatus } from '../../services/partnerService';
import {
  PageHeader, Tabs, Card, Badge, Button, EmptyState, Modal, tk,
} from '../../components/ui';
import { alertErr, alertOk } from '../../lib/apiError';

const TONE = { pending: 'amber', confirmed: 'blue', completed: 'green', cancelled: 'red' };

export default function Bookings() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState(null);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('all');
  const [view, setView] = useState(null);
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState(null);

  const load = () => listBookings().then(setItems).catch(alertErr);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    if (!items) return [];
    return tab === 'all' ? items : items.filter((b) => b.status === tab);
  }, [items, tab]);

  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    const c = { all: items?.length || 0 };
    (items || []).forEach((b) => { c[b.status] = (c[b.status] || 0) + 1; });
    return c;
  }, [items]);

  async function act(booking, status, label) {
    setBusyId(booking.id);
    try {
      await updateBookingStatus(booking.id, status);
      toast.success(label);
      load();
    } catch (err) { alertErr(err); }
    finally { setBusyId(null); setView(null); }
  }

  const TABS = [
    { value: 'all', label: 'All', count: counts.all },
    { value: 'pending', label: 'Pending', count: counts.pending },
    { value: 'confirmed', label: 'Confirmed', count: counts.confirmed },
    { value: 'completed', label: 'Completed', count: counts.completed },
    { value: 'cancelled', label: 'Cancelled', count: counts.cancelled },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Bookings" desc="Customer booking manage করো" />
      <Tabs tabs={TABS} value={tab} onChange={setTab} />

      <div className="mt-4">
        {!items ? (
          <Card className="p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-400">Loading…</Card>
        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState icon={Eye} title="কোনো booking নেই"
            desc="এই filter-এ কোনো booking পাওয়া যায়নি।" />
        ) : (
          <Card className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full min-w-[720px] text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Booking</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Customer</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Date</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Amount</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Status</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-medium">Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {filtered.map((bk) => (
                  <tr key={bk.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/60">
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <p className="font-medium text-slate-800">{bk.booking_code}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{bk.service?.name || '—'}</p>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <p className="text-slate-700">{bk.customer_name}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{bk.customer_phone || ''}</p>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-slate-600">{bk.travel_date}<br /><span className="text-xs text-slate-400">{bk.guests} guests</span></td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-slate-800">{tk(bk.amount)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3"><Badge tone={TONE[bk.status]}>{bk.status}</Badge></td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <div className="flex justify-end gap-1.5">
                        <Button variant="ghost" size="xs" onClick={() => setView(bk)}><Eye size={13} /> View</Button>
                        {bk.status === 'pending' && (
                          <>
                            <Button size="xs" loading={busyId === bk.id}
                              onClick={() => act(bk, 'confirmed', 'Booking confirmed ✓')}>
                              <Check size={13} /> Accept
                            </Button>
                            <Button variant="danger" size="xs" loading={busyId === bk.id}
                              onClick={() => act(bk, 'cancelled', 'Booking declined')}>
                              <X size={13} /> Reject
                            </Button>
                          </>
                        )}
                        {bk.status === 'confirmed' && (
                          <Button size="xs" loading={busyId === bk.id}
                            onClick={() => act(bk, 'completed', 'Marked completed ✓')}>
                            <CheckCircle2 size={13} /> Complete
                          </Button>
                        )}
                        {bk.customer_phone && (
                          <a href={`https://wa.me/${bk.customer_phone.replace(/\D/g, '')}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                            <Button variant="outline" size="xs"><MessageCircle size={13} /> Chat</Button>
                          </a>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </Card>
        )}
      </div>

      <Modal open={!!view} onClose={() => setView(null)} title={`Booking ${view?.booking_code || ''}`}>
        {view && (
          <div className="space-y-2 text-sm">
            {[
              ['Customer', view.customer_name],
              ['Phone', view.customer_phone || '—'],
              ['Service', view.service?.name || '—'],
              ['Date', view.travel_date],
              ['Guests', view.guests],
              ['Amount', tk(view.amount)],
              ['Payment', view.payment_status],
              ['Notes', view.notes || '—'],
            ].map(([k, v]) => (
              <div key={k} className="flex justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-2">
                <span className="text-slate-500">{k}</span>
                <span className="text-right font-medium text-slate-800">{v}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
            {view.status === 'pending' && (
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-3">
                <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => act(view, 'cancelled', 'Declined')}><X size={14} /> Reject</Button>
                <Button onClick={() => act(view, 'confirmed', 'Confirmed ✓')}><Check size={14} /> Accept</Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>
    </>
  );
}
```
